# v2026.9.7

AI agents and tools can read these release notes as [plain Markdown](https://raw.githubusercontent.com/openclaw/openclaw/main/CHANGELOG/2026.9.7.md).

v2026.9.7 makes longer conversations easier to read and manage, with less repeated work as replies stream and clearer previews of shared drafts. The [Mac app](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/webchat) brings the web conversation into full chat windows, while the optional [Agents API runtime](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes) gives agents a hosted workspace for their work.

Before upgrading, review the migration steps for Tasks and TaskFlow, Tool Search code mode, and custom streaming clients.

**Release scale:** 2,669 pull requests, 482 direct commits, and 326 contributors.

## Installation and Onboarding

Setup gives clearer guidance from the first prompt to a connected agent, with fixes for account selection, pairing, and installations that could appear successful but fail to start.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Get through setup with clearer choices">

[Onboarding](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/onboard) reaches its first question sooner, with a shorter banner animation and less work loading setup screens you will not use. When no Gateway is running, explicitly skipping service installation also avoids an unnecessary startup wait. Invalid options and workspace paths now produce more useful errors, while Ask OpenClaw opens and focuses the destination chat when you leave setup assistance.

For a workspace without generated starter files, `openclaw setup --baseline --skip-bootstrap` now remembers that choice on later runs and preserves existing files. A conflicting value supplied by an exact configuration include must be changed in its owning file. Remote onboarding also checks a supplied `--workspace` locally, so omit an unused invalid path.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Load only the selected guided, classic, or unattended setup flow [#160169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160169).
- Shorten the animated banner before the first setup prompt [#160250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160250).
- Use one authenticated reachability check when unattended setup explicitly skips daemon installation, retaining health checks for a running Gateway [#160168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160168).

**Bug fixes**

- Honor and remember baseline skip-bootstrap choices while preserving existing files and shared configuration includes [275d0d4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/275d0d497bf27daf0c908597522b5f7ce4827842). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Preserve numbers, booleans, objects, and null in plugin setup choices without losing neighboring numeric-keyed settings [c762b23](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c762b23736449d9de045d052acaba26bcd3e21e7). Thanks @moerai.
- Keep temporary model-verification runs out of ordinary chat and session events [#155777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155777). Thanks @mertbasar0, @obviyus, and @axiom-ncis.
- Open and focus the destination chat after an Ask OpenClaw handoff, without transferring the conversation or starting work [#155907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155907). Thanks @romneyda.
- Report unknown or incomplete root options instead of opening setup or terminal chat [#159213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159213).
- Skip startup polling when interactive service installation is declined and no temporary Gateway exists; managed starts and explicit Gateway reuse retain their waits [#160307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160307).
- Reject unusable workspace paths before reset or setup, and identify workspace or Gateway failures without blaming successful AI access [#160373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160373).

**Documentation**

- Clarify setup prerequisites, caller-supplied example inputs, and links to pairing, memory-provider, and recovery instructions [4c9869c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4c9869c6fd012b94123f0cae4554e1d605c5c846). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Show `setup --baseline` in the CLI help example for unattended baseline setup [#159214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159214).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect the intended model account">

First-run provider setup restores styled cards and detected API-key or token choices, with clearer labels for different sign-in methods. Selecting a manual credential option opens the form; **Connect & verify** submits it and can set the agent's active model. Device-code sign-in no longer loses setup to the shorter eight-minute activation timer, though the provider's own code expiry still applies. Custom model addresses must use HTTP or HTTPS, and automated [LM Studio setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/lmstudio) now returns readable JSON errors when connection or model checks fail.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep setup activation alive through the provider's device-code sign-in window [#156440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156440).
- Restore first-run provider cards, detected manual credentials, and distinct login choices [#159412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159412). Thanks @stevenlee-oai.
- Return structured LM Studio setup failures with recovery guidance, a nonzero exit status, and no application of the proposed provider settings [#160196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160196).
- Reject malformed or non-HTTP(S) custom model addresses before interactive credential selection and verification [#160301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160301).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Pair and reconnect devices">

A Gateway behind a public HTTPS proxy can use its configured public address for [device join links and local QR setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/devices). Browser access also inherits that address when its allowed-origin list is omitted, including in existing configurations. Explicit lists still take precedence. Check any other browser addresses before removing an existing list, and verify mapped localhost dashboard access in container setups when relying on this default. Used or expired dashboard links now explain how to get a fresh link with `openclaw dashboard` on the Gateway host, and valid manual credentials can work immediately after the rejected link. These links remain single-use and expire after ten minutes.

Android adds **Request all** for eligible optional permissions, while keeping each allow or deny choice and letting setup finish after denied SMS access. Granting camera or location access still leaves their feature switches under your control. Notification-listener access remains a separate Settings step, and SMS requires a supported third-party build, a telephony device, and Gateway approval; Google Play builds do not support SMS.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Inherit `gateway.publicOrigin` for browser access only when `allowedOrigins` is omitted; explicit lists, including an empty list, override inheritance without disabling other local-origin exceptions, and public-origin changes revoke browser connections no longer allowed [#157170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157170).
- Request eligible Android permissions together without automatically enabling camera or location, and allow setup to finish after SMS denial [#159727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159727). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Show commands to reconnect a previously paired node when `openclaw connect` has no target; the user still runs the commands, and stale pairing needs fresh enrollment [#159810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159810).

**Bug fixes**

- Use the configured public address as the loopback pairing fallback while preserving explicit, remote, Tailscale, and LAN routes; remote QR setup retains its separate prerequisites [#159465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159465).
- Describe rejected Android and Apple setup codes as no longer valid, with fresh-code guidance instead of assuming expiration [#159745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159745). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Distinguish partial SMS read/send access and explain when Android restrictions may prevent granting the missing permission [#159747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159747). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Preserve pending node pairing requests when unchanged permission keys arrive in a different order [#159759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159759).
- Show denied users administrator role guidance instead of describing a reachable Gateway as unavailable [#159948](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159948).
- Clear rejected dashboard pairing tokens before manual-credential retries and explain fresh-link recovery, including `openclaw dashboard --json` when browser or clipboard access is unavailable [#160684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160684).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Configure channels and settings">

Setup chat can now remove an individual configuration override so inherited defaults apply without replacing neighboring settings. The change still requires exact setup approval or the requesting conversation's permissions; remote rescue messages cannot perform it, so use your regular agent or the local CLI there. For unattended [channel setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/channels), error guidance now points to options the selected channel actually accepts instead of recommending an unsupported flag.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Give channel-specific help when noninteractive setup cannot use `--use-env`, while keeping guided setup's interactive-terminal requirement [#157551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157551). Thanks @sxh313 and @obviyus.
- Let the Gateway handle other work while channel setup checks policy after selecting an agent, with configuration ownership rechecked before proceeding [#158783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158783).
- Remove approved configuration overrides through setup or delegated chat, preserve sibling settings, and refuse remote rescue deletion [#158873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158873). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Install on the selected platform and runtime">

Fresh npm installs include the missing dependency that could prevent OpenClaw and Doctor from starting. Existing services using a supported Bun executable also retain it during an implicit reinstall or update refresh, unless an explicit runtime choice, pin, or wrapper takes precedence. On a Bun-only machine, automatic installation can use the supported Bun running the CLI; Node remains the fresh-install default when available. The [Bun compatibility guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility) explains those choices.

Windows installations now report failure when the command is still missing from PATH, including a nonzero result for automation. Open a new terminal and run `openclaw doctor` as the installer advises. For FreeBSD, the [installation guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/installer) clarifies that native Codex lacks a FreeBSD binary. Its documented OpenAI API alternative requires an API-key profile and explicit OpenClaw runtime selection, with API billing separate from a ChatGPT or Codex subscription. Team administrators also get an ordered [team server deployment guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/team-server).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Report an unusable Windows PATH as an installation failure, preserving catchable PowerShell scriptblock errors and recovery guidance [#134406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134406). Thanks @ly85206559 and @fuller-stack-dev.
- Select source-worker loaders from the running runtime's identity when reusing a renamed Bun or Node executable [#158521](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158521).
- Bundle the required Undici dependency alongside patched Proxyline so fresh npm installs and staged update candidates can start [#160015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160015).
- Retain supported recorded Bun service runtimes during reinstalls and update refreshes, with an automatic Bun-only first-install fallback [#160189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160189).

**Documentation**

- Explain FreeBSD's native Codex limitation, explicit API runtime selection, and separate API billing [#155980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155980). Thanks @vincentkoc and @lucarinaorg.
- Add a Linux team-server walkthrough covering Cloudflare sign-in, identities, roles, public session links, backups, and verification [#157085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157085).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Build and recover a source checkout">

Source users can build and lint from a checkout nested inside another Node.js project, using the checkout's own frozen dependencies. Completed failed builds release their locks for another attempt, and cancelling `pnpm openclaw` during a rebuild prevents the CLI from starting afterward. Active build work still settles before its locks are released.

The development compiler now uses a prerelease with native packages for macOS arm64/x64, Linux arm/arm64/x64, and Windows arm64/x64. Source-build packages previously available for BSD, AIX, SunOS, and other Linux architectures are absent, so check your build host before upgrading the source toolchain. This build-host restriction is separate from the platforms on which an installed OpenClaw runtime can run.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Finish Linux help generation when owned descendants have exited but await reaping, while still refusing live or unobservable work [#158448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158448). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Explain uncomparable installer versions before replacement and reject linked plugin-build output paths that could redirect writes or cleanup outside `dist` [#159199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159199).
- Release source-build ownership after completed failures and reject invalid build arguments without starting work [#160059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160059).
- Honor cancellation during source rebuilds and lock waits, without launching the CLI or recording cancelled preparation as complete [#160172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160172). Thanks @romneyda, @vyctorbrzezowski, and @jalehman.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Support nested source builds with checkout-owned declaration inputs, using TypeScript `7.1.0-dev.20260917.1` and its narrower native build-host coverage [#158674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158674).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Web UI

The [Control UI](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) makes longer conversations easier to read and manage, with clearer controls for shared work and more ways to recover unfinished drafts.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Find and organize conversations">

The sidebar’s [Filter & sort panel](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) keeps filtering and display choices together while you adjust them. Pinned conversations stay in Pages when showing all agents, and saved forks remain beneath their parent after work finishes or OpenClaw restarts. Unnamed heartbeat sessions stay out of the ordinary conversation list; Show system sessions still reveals them.

<Accordion title="Start chats with the intended settings">

You can opt new chats into the selected agent’s [configured model and reasoning defaults](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-gateway) with `gateway.controlUi.newSessionModelDefaults: "configured"`. Remembering the last selection remains the default, and deliberate choices for an unsent draft survive reload. Update the Gateway and UI together, then refresh or reconnect before enabling the option. New Session also waits for current agent settings while preserving your text and chosen folder.

Guests granted [session-write access](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/operator-scopes) can create conversations and use permitted controls on their own chats. Full permission mode, sandbox changes, and changing an existing conversation’s context window still require an administrator. Simultaneous first messages in the same empty shared conversation no longer produce the specific false branch-change rejection.

<Accordion title="Keep your place in long conversations">

Long, tool-heavy conversations do less repeated browser work when replies stream, you switch chats, or older messages load. Scrolling and late-loading content better preserve whether you are following the latest reply or reading earlier messages. [Chat tables](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) wrap more readably, use available width, and put Copy and Expand below their content; dense tables can still scroll sideways. Consecutive tool activity from resumed work stays together in an expandable log, with answers and other visible messages keeping their own place.

<Accordion title="Receive incremental live replies">

Live replies send newly appended text instead of repeatedly transmitting the whole answer. Updated bundled clients reconstruct the conversation and use available history when they need to recover missing text. This reduces repeated traffic, especially for longer replies.

Third-party raw clients must follow the [updated streaming contract](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-bootstrap-and-events). The protocol still reports version 4, but later frames can omit the former full-text fields without negotiating that change. Custom SDK transports must also preserve event and acknowledgment order or end retired streams. Recovery remains bounded and can report that complete history is unavailable.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Send incremental live text and require raw-client reconstruction and reconnect migration [#158587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158587).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Restore the right conversation after reconnect">

Reopened and split conversations restore their own history and catch up independently, and other tabs follow branch switches or rewinds without losing unsent drafts. Saved reply drafts keep their selected quote. Ambiguous older split panes ask you to choose a conversation instead of guessing its agent; brief transcript rebuilds can recover, while persistent failures still need another attempt.

Confirmed Remove keeps a queued prompt hidden after reconnect, but retains its stored content and cancellation record; Incognito keeps its cancellation notice until the private session ends. Stop remains separate. If a stopped reply cannot be saved, [copy its visible text before leaving](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect).

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<Accordion title="Keep background chats from crowding the connection">

Background sidebar chats now use short, paced previews while the open conversation keeps its full reply stream. Hidden tabs release sidebar-only activity subscriptions, and session lists reuse current details instead of repeatedly requesting the same information. Temporary narration failures and cleanup requests use backoff, with retries stopping when their session or connection is no longer relevant.

When upgrading custom SDK clients, [update the Gateway alongside the SDK](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-bootstrap-and-events). New subscription requests include fields older Gateways can reject even after a successful version-4 handshake. This subscription change keeps full-rate delivery for older clients; the raw-client streaming migration above still applies.

<Accordion title="Steer or stop work while keeping your draft">

Eligible corrections can [steer active work](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) even when an older message must wait as a follow-up. Equal-permission participants can also steer shared turns. Running tools are not interrupted by steering, and different permissions or unsupported runtimes still require a follow-up. In a mixed-person turn, the agent must explicitly select whose [screen](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/screen) or [theme](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/theme) to change; other personal settings require a new personal turn. The original owner’s authority, approvals, and provider account remain in use. Incognito and cloud-worker turns do not gain steering through this change.

Stop stays beside Send, Queue, or Steer while you draft, preserving unsent text and attachments. If history reveals that Stop was targeting an inactive response, the next explicit Stop click can target the current one. Stalled attachment preparation also leaves a removable error so you can keep and send your text without the failed file.

<Accordion title="Read work, questions and progress at a glance">

Completed agent narration stays inline with its formatting and tool activity, while work summaries show total calls and distinguish skipped tools from failures or approval blocks. Keep commentary controls what remains after the run. [Progress cards](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/progress-card) preserve their opening state through sends and completion within the current page and connection, and users with write access can dismiss a card in any state. Dismissal clears saved progress without stopping the agent; a later update can create another card.

<Accordion title="Keep Goal actions and recovery tied to the right session">

[Goal changes](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/goal) made while a conversation is opening stay with that conversation and its Check outcome controls. Rejected actions and failed outcome checks explain the problem instead of appearing unresponsive. Opening a new Goal also keeps an older failed message in its own Retry entry, and mobile controls remain above long objectives.

<Accordion title="Edit your personal instructions">

On eligible shared Gateways, you can edit your [personal instructions](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model) in Profile or ask an authenticated chat to update them. This edits your own file in a permitted local agent workspace without granting general file access. It requires at least two distinct unmerged person profiles, excluding shared Owner; separate files require individual sign-in, while single-user setups keep using the root `USER.md`. The 4,000-character cap and prompt limits remain. Conflicting saves preserve your draft, but drafts are not guaranteed to survive closing the browser, and independent host-editor conflict detection remains best effort.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Edit personal instructions in Profile settings or chat [#155256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155256). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Correct settings without losing edits">

Settings explains definite validation rejection, keeps your edits available, and offers Show reason, Retry, and [Discard draft and reload](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings). Discard removes **all unsaved configuration edits**, not just the invalid field. Unknown Save or Apply outcomes keep their recovery controls and stay tied to the original Gateway, so return there to retry or explicitly discard the draft. A saved change may still need Apply or the displayed restart instructions before it takes effect. Passive refreshes also keep plugin settings editable and focused.

<Accordion title="Edit and review files without losing drafts">

When an update disables the editor while unsaved files block refresh, [Review file drafts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect) lets you copy or download each draft, identify its original conversation and pane, and keep or explicitly discard it. Copying alone keeps reload protection in place. These drafts remain in the tab’s memory and are lost on manual browser reload or tab closure; recovery does not write files back to OpenClaw. Incognito drafts receive similar review-before-refresh protection without persistent storage.

File editing preserves multiline paste and line endings, catches competing creation of agent files, and refreshes Review after a confirmed save. Incomplete Review results no longer claim there are no changes. Registered remote workspaces can supply read-only previews from the paired host; saving and local reveal remain unavailable there.

<Accordion title="Read, reuse and prepare attachments">

Eligible [PDF attachments](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) open beside the conversation in Files, retaining page and zoom when an unchanged document’s link renews. Preview supports same-origin PDFs up to 16 MiB through the browser’s native reader; unavailable or oversized previews offer the original download, and external PDFs and Office documents remain download-only. Image filenames and media-only reply context also survive the move into saved history, while expanded assistant videos can be browsed within the same turn.

Oversized static PNGs can be [resized locally](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/webchat) to the existing upload limit, preserving transparency and aspect ratio. Sources are limited to 25 megapixels; oversized animated PNGs are rejected rather than flattened, and other formats keep their limits. Previously viewed images can reuse a valid bounded cache when you scroll back.

<Accordion title="Preview a photo before attaching it">

Take photo in Chat and New Session now opens a [local camera preview](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui), with Capture, Retake, camera selection, and Use photo. Confirmation attaches the still to your draft without sending it, and preview requests no microphone. Live preview requires HTTPS or localhost and camera permission. Unsupported or insecure contexts offer the device picker; after permission denial, use Retry or Upload photo.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Preview, capture and confirm camera photos; permission denial offers retry or upload rather than native capture [#160253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160253).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Copy conversations and open links your way">

You can copy your own prompts as Markdown, and conversation exports omit empty speaker sections while leaving unknown authors labeled Message. The optional [Open links outside OpenClaw setting](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) also covers browser-preview images and Open buttons, with an alternate Open in OpenClaw action. It is off by default and stored locally per Gateway in that browser or app webview, so it does not sync across devices. Internal navigation, downloads, and explicit menu choices retain their behavior.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Copy user messages as Markdown [#156563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156563). Thanks @vacinc.
- Choose to open links outside OpenClaw [#159887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159887). Thanks @vacinc.
- Keep GitHub item identity in failed link previews [#160219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160219). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Improve GitHub reference contrast, focus, and wrapping in chat [72b76842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/72b76842012f405400a2b6b694f46bd62b7bca1c). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Preserve typing focus while GitHub documents load [#157202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157202).
- Omit empty sections from conversation exports [#157590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157590).
- Preserve repository context in GitHub folder-link labels [#157619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157619). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Use neutral labels for messages without recorded authors [#160257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160257). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Make browser preview cards follow external-opening preferences [#160410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160410). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Ask a side question without disturbing the main chat">

[Side chat](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) accepts dropped or pasted images for the current question and preserves newer drafts while earlier answers or Clear requests finish. Image questions require an image-capable utility model; unsupported images receive an explanation and explicit Retry after you choose a suitable model. Images are not retained in restored text history, so reattach them for later questions. Failed-turn images last only while their pane remains retained.

Compatible CLI installations without direct provider credentials can use a fresh, bounded-context, text-only fallback. That route has no tools or image support, while working direct-provider routes keep their existing capabilities. Explicit utility selections remain respected.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Ask Side chat about dropped or pasted images [#156840](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156840). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Remove automatic summaries from Side chat [#156892](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156892). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

**Bug fixes**

- Explain unsupported Side chat images and preserve Retry [#156900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156900). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.
- Preserve Side chat input during delayed updates [#156990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156990).
- Offer Side chat fallback without direct API credentials [#157474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157474). Thanks @rogeriochaves, @obviyus, @sidboswell.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find and inspect the selected model">

Large model lists require less repeated rendering while searching, and the chat picker opens the selected model’s provider section automatically. Search follows the interface’s reading direction, with the full name available in the open picker or accessible label. Cmd+K keeps provider-refresh diagnostics on model-management pages; an empty model result can therefore lack the former warning, although direct search failures still appear.

[Usage](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/feature-reference) keeps previous totals visible while refreshing and offers scoped retry after failure. Large-session refreshes avoid loading the entire transcript at once; [Doctor](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor) reports incomplete totals, which you retry by requesting usage again.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Group Decision model choices by provider [#155301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155301). Thanks @jalehman.
- Keep model caches across unrelated metadata updates [#157254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157254).
- Remove routine model-picker hover tooltips [#158697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158697). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Reduce pauses when searching large model lists [#159905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159905). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Show model search results while other categories load [#151022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151022). Thanks @obviyus.
- Bound memory use when refreshing large-session usage totals [#156937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156937). Thanks @conan-scott.
- Keep usage totals visible through background refresh [#157270](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157270). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep metadata and model lists available during unrelated session changes [#158576](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158576).
- Align model-picker search text with reading direction [#159937](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159937). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Show the selected model when opening the chat model picker [#160132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160132).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See who is connected and what is running">

[Online presence](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/presence) distinguishes Active within two minutes of interaction, Idle afterward, and Online when activity is unknown. Quiet reading can become idle; these states describe recent OpenClaw use, not attention or physical presence. Your own account now appears even when you are alone. Open and Running counts cover accessible conversations owned by the displayed people across agents, with sorting and a running-only filter. Running means direct live work, excluding queued and descendant-only activity; unknown counts show a dash.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce database work for human-mention suggestions [#158481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158481).
- Reduce repeated presence work for concurrent viewers [#158497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158497).
- Reuse prepared assistant identities and prevent stale avatar cache replacement [#158597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158597).
- Reduce unrelated typing-state scans and expire idle groups [#158649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158649).
- Reduce presence traffic when many tabs connect [#158942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158942).
- Distinguish online connectivity from recent person activity [#159493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159493).
- Show open and running conversations beside online people [#160022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160022).
- Reduce presence traffic during browser join bursts [#160150](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160150).

**Bug fixes**

- Align chat mention avatars with names [#155969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155969). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Recover chat viewer presence after a lost acknowledgement [#156255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156255). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Publish client presence after the handshake succeeds [#159554](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159554).
- Include your own account in the Online sidebar [#160618](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160618). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow shared drafts without filling the screen">

Busy shared chats keep [two full draft previews](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) in arrival order and summarize additional active typers in one compact row. Full previews change to Draft after ten seconds of inactivity and expire after thirty seconds; paused overflow drafts are hidden. Updates retain the latest text up to four times per second, and resumed typing keeps the same bubble. These previews are temporary, unsent browser state rather than saved messages.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Pace collaborator previews and discard obsolete queued drafts [#158552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158552).
- Simplify collaborator draft labels and idle expiry [#159235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159235). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.
- Keep collaborator typing previews compact in busy chats [#159263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159263). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski, @shakkernerd.

**Bug fixes**

- Keep collaborator drafts visible during typing pauses [#157100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157100). Thanks @patrick-erichsen.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Load the right identity and profile picture">

[Profile](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model) explains your current connection’s access and offers Reconnect after administrator changes. Guests with limited session access can load and refresh their own identity without opening the People roster or gaining editing rights. Avatar downloads still have separate permissions, so some guests see initials. Sign-in screens handle long hostnames more cleanly, and saved avatar requests share bounded work while retaining overload limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Improve sign-in pages, disabled-button styling, Mac reconnect and voice controls, and Approvals layout [#157251](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157251).
- Load saved avatars through workers, retaining the distinct-profile overload limit [#158445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158445).

**Bug fixes**

- Reuse saved avatars and return retryable overload responses; automatic browser retry is not added [#158676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158676).
- Keep long hostnames inside the sign-in card [#159899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159899). Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Show guests their own Control UI profile [#160503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160503). Thanks @shakkernerd.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Notice completions, mentions and approvals">

Completed visible conversations can gain an unread marker when work finishes after your last read. Inbox mentions and update cards use consistent ages, expansion, and dismissal controls, while approval details stay tied to the current Gateway. [Browser notification settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/notifications) no longer wait behind the specific background conversation-access preparation that could stall registration and preference reads.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Align Inbox notification ages and controls [#156560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156560). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Batch Web Push recipient preparation [#158595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158595).

**Bug fixes**

- Mark newly completed visible sessions unread [#155690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155690). Thanks @hxy91819.
- Keep approval state scoped to the current Gateway and omit undiscovered model setup options [#158032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158032).
- Reschedule mention expiry after clock rollback [#158614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158614).
- Keep chat subscriptions stable during unrelated approval activity [#158633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158633).
- Keep notification settings responsive during background preparation [#159709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159709).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Review Activity and today’s sessions">

The Activity Sessions tab gains a [Today overview](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) that follows your filters and counts matching sessions beyond the first displayed page. Each session contributes once at its latest activity hour, and incomplete people counts show a lower bound. Eligible visible agent-created chats can also receive recaps.

Live Activity now collects only while its live view and browser tab are visible, across up to 100 authorized active sessions. It cannot backfill time spent elsewhere, and leaving the page resets the next visit. Raw Debug history is separate and starts only while Debug or expanded System busyness is open; its retained events disappear when the last diagnostic view closes.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid unnecessary Activity rendering outside Live [#156206](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156206).
- Reduce Live Activity rendering overhead [#157952](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157952).
- Reuse resident recap facts; matching keys in multiple stores can still select the wrong store [#158803](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158803).
- Add a Today overview to Activity sessions [#159611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159611).

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve Activity recaps across session-list refreshes [8cea9e99](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8cea9e995769d86ec33b36d60bcdf82f5e092b60). Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Publish ready Activity recaps during unrelated refreshes [#154907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154907).
- Generate Activity recaps for visible agent-created chats [#159458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159458).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Capture raw Debug events only while diagnostics are open, with no earlier-event lookback [#158533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158533).
- Capture Live Activity only while visible, without off-page backfill or retention across page visits [#158574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158574).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Review the working branch and pull request">

[Session PR previews](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) follow the working branch, so an incidental PR link in conversation no longer creates a banner. Branch and CI previews disappear on detached or default-branch checkouts; recorded publication results remain separate. Idle sidebar rows reuse last-known summaries without driving Git polling, so unopened sessions can lack badges after a fresh load or reconnect until viewed. Mobile chat also shows the working branch before a PR exists.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce repeated work in pull-request updates [#158302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158302).
- Reuse unchanged worktree branch suggestions [#158399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158399).
- Skip redundant Git ancestry checks for matching branch tips [#159301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159301).

**Bug fixes**

- Recover stalled pull-request status subscriptions [#156310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156310). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Release PR-panel caches when preparing viewers leave [#157776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157776).
- Show the working branch in mobile chat [#160597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160597). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Show branch-owned PR banners only; detached and default checkouts no longer retain them [#158578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158578). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Continue a conversation after a recoverable worker failure">

After an eligible worker failure, sending another message can [continue the same conversation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-sessions) once cleanup and saved-workspace recovery finish. The replacement uses the original paired device or cloud profile and retains history and the last accepted workspace. The interrupted turn is not replayed, and unsynced files from a lost worker may be lost. Setup failures, unavailable destinations, or unfinished recovery still need their specific recovery action; use Restart session to choose another destination.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Continue conversations after recoverable worker failures [a7ded174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a7ded174bd966dcbc78e5eae1c059b65b5ecb80c).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Inspect cloud worker readiness and capacity">

Administrators can inspect [Cloud workers → Pool](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/warm-images) under Settings → Connections to see ready, preparing, releasing, and attention-needed workers alongside occupied reserve slots. Cleanup can keep a consumed worker’s slot occupied. The visible view refreshes every ten seconds and retains the last result with a warning if refresh fails. Profiles remains the place to change limits.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show cloud worker readiness and occupied capacity in Settings [#157200](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157200).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Return to dashboards, desktops and widgets">

Returning to a chat can reveal its existing assigned desktop or still-live browser tab, while remembering dismissal in the current browser profile. Ordinary [dashboard gallery navigation](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/dashboards) preserves retained widget interactions, but browser reload still clears unsaved in-memory state. Self-contained [HTML and SVG widgets](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/show-widget) can now use up to 10 MiB including wrapper overhead. Registered-source limits and Discord’s 48 KiB HTML limit remain separate, and larger documents consume more storage and rendering memory.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reveal existing desktops and browsers when returning to chat [b8929c45](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b8929c456559af2ea7d1b0d366c4e8df53a13798). Thanks @vincentkoc, Peter Steinberger.
- Allow wrapped HTML and SVG widgets up to 10 MiB while retaining smaller registered-source and Discord limits [#159970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159970).

- Align desktop headers with sidebar controls [#156432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156432). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep HTML widget padding when maximized [#157203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157203).
- Keep session previews available during unchanged metadata updates [#157943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157943).
- Correct Desktop panel fullscreen ownership [#160277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160277).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep browser voice sessions usable">

Browser Talk can continue through brief relay stalls instead of ending the call immediately. Excess pending microphone audio is still dropped, with a visible notice asking you to repeat the affected speech; an unresponsive relay retains its existing timeout.

The browser composer’s direct voice dropdown is removed. Choose future-call defaults in [Settings → Talk](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions), or ask the assistant to change the current call’s voice. Settings does not directly switch an active call, and native iOS and Android controls are unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Talk calls running through brief stalls and explain dropped speech [#155839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155839). Thanks @marvinthebored, @peetiegonzalez, @obviyus.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Remove the browser in-call voice dropdown; Settings chooses future defaults and the assistant can change an active call [#159441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159441).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep plugin settings and installation status in view">

Installed plugin results in the command palette open their overview directly, including disabled plugins without enabling them. Sidebar selection follows navigation and browser history, and an open installation-status popup stays visible during a refresh of the same catalog selection. Plugins Advanced can retry a missing settings schema after connection or a transient failure using Reload.

<Accordion title="Use consistent colors and clear controls">

Menus, buttons, context warnings, embedded apps, and Desktop viewer margins follow the selected palette more consistently. Keyboard users gain clearer focus, shortcut hints, and access to specific sidebar, file, media, and timeline controls. Forced-colors indicators remain visible in the covered controls; imported palettes keep their supplied contrast.

<Accordion title="Keep mobile chat controls within reach">

Phone layouts give session titles more room and align chat, New Session, menus, and navigation with the browser’s reported safe areas and keyboard geometry. The empty New Session message box stays within reach, while recent chats remain available in the sidebar. Filter sheets stay open after taps on headings or padding, and delayed command refreshes preserve valid argument suggestions. [Mobile layout guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/connect-and-pair) explains how keyboard placement follows the space reported by your browser.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

- Align mobile margins and keyboard-aware safe areas [#160388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160388). Thanks @hxy91819.
- Keep mobile filters open and preserve command suggestions [#160600](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160600). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Load the interface with less startup work">

The interface loads more page-specific content when you open that page, reducing its startup work. Automatic chat titles wait until a reply begins or its initial attempt finishes, so they do not compete with preparing the first response. The diagram renderer returns to Mermaid 11.17.2, so Mermaid 12-only diagram families and built-in ELK layout are unavailable and connector geometry may differ.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

</AccordionGroup>

## Updates and Maintenance

Updates and repair tools give clearer results and handle more interrupted maintenance paths. Several older interfaces are retired, with migration steps below.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Move from Tasks and TaskFlow to native controls">

Tasks and TaskFlow are removed, including their CLI commands, APIs, SDK interfaces, and chat panels. Use [native subagent controls](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/tool-reference), ACP sessions, background-command process IDs, Cron history, or ordinary Lobster pipelines for the work each owns. Workboard, generated-media completion, session transcripts, and ordinary progress cards remain. Subagent wait and cancel requests now use `runId` and `runIds` instead of `taskId` and `taskIds`.

Plugin authors must replace the [removed Task interfaces](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/removed-surfaces); there is no compatibility layer. The TaskFlow Webhooks endpoints also have no equivalent HTTP replacement, although ordinary Gateway hooks remain. After direct binary replacement, run `openclaw doctor --fix` before starting the Gateway. Existing Task and Flow records remain, and Doctor imports eligible Codex assignments only when their ownership can be established. Follow the [migration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) for unresolved records and review the explicit plugin permissions retained when stale Webhooks settings are repaired. If no allowed plugins remain, repair disables plugins until you choose an allowlist and re-enable them; conflicting plugin aliases need an explicit choice of IDs.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

Earlier Tasks-specific changes below describe work superseded by the final retirement. Their source records remain included for the complete change list.

<Accordion title="Migrate Tool Search to structured controls">

[Tool Search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tool-search) now uses structured search, description, and call controls. Setting `tools.toolSearch` to `true` selects those controls; disabled configurations stay disabled. The separate `tool_search_code` tool, `mode: "code"`, and `codeTimeoutMs` setting are retired. Normal updates run Doctor migration, but manual upgrades or deferred repair require `openclaw doctor --fix` before restart because unmigrated settings prevent startup. Use the existing [Code Mode](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode) for JavaScript orchestration. New nested call IDs use `tool_call`; existing transcript IDs are preserved.

<Accordion title="Prepare older installations before upgrading">

Installations older than June 2026 must follow the [2026.9.5 bridge upgrade](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#upgrading-very-old-versions) before installing the latest version. Make and verify a backup, stop the Gateway through its actual service owner, and keep the same account, installation prefix, profile, and state paths. Install 2026.9.5, run `openclaw doctor --fix`, and verify the needed imports. Required plugins must be installed and relevant channels or accounts enabled for their imports, with the Gateway stopped while making temporary changes. If a newer version has already migrated the databases, follow the compatible-backup and downgrade guidance before using the older bridge.

General legacy repair now belongs to Doctor. Manual binary replacements must arrange that repair before startup; official container entrypoints run it automatically, while custom entrypoints need an explicit Doctor step. Current Doctor preserves retired source files and settings rather than importing every historical format. A deferred repair is still pending work, so resolve its reported blocker and rerun `openclaw update repair`.

- Preserve and flush legacy workspace setup archives correctly on Windows [#156704](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156704)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Move legacy repair to Doctor, with automatic activation in official containers [2a1402cd3e39](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2a1402cd3e3914352820a24961087f1de0c736d9)
- Require the 2026.9.5 bridge for pre-June installations; remove MCP attachments_fetch limit and its history fallback [#156285](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156285)
- Migrate retired heartbeat, llm, embeddedPi, embeddedHarness, and sandbox.perSession settings through 2026.9.5 [#156859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156859)
- Retire pre-June channel-state and shared credentials/oauth.json imports with the 2026.9.5 bridge; retain supported auth JSON imports [#157165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157165)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve backups and restore eligible configuration changes">

New [verified backups](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/backup) record the databases captured and reject missing or mismatched entries. Coverage includes discovered unregistered, moved, and custom agent databases and committed WAL data. Older archives without that inventory have unknown completeness, and a config-only export excludes included files and other state. Make and verify a backup before updating. Eligible package rollback can restore database snapshots only when capture occurred with the Gateway stopped, the candidate never started, and subsequent writes remain accounted for. [Included configuration files](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery) can also be restored when Doctor's writes and the original paths are verified; later edits or incomplete capture prevent automatic restoration.

The updated updater removes its own database snapshots after verified successful activation. Failed, rolled-back, or unverified updates retain them and may leave the service stopped for recovery. Older snapshots need manual review of the update reports before removal; Doctor reports them but never deletes them, even with `--fix`. Capture still needs substantial free space and can refuse even a schema-compatible update before migration. Installing this release does not add missing snapshots or new cleanup behavior to an older updater already running.

<Accordion title="Recover interrupted package updates">

Supported POSIX npm updates now print an external Node recovery command before replacing the package. If the updater disappears, that helper can inspect or finish the recorded package and launcher replacement using the original operation identity. Follow the printed command with other package managers stopped, then explicitly start and verify the Gateway. This [recovery path](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) does not restore all state, repair plugins, or restart the service, and it does not cover Windows, native pnpm/Bun layouts, or execution under Bun.

Supported staged packages can perform their own compatibility checks, with `--admission installed` available to use the installed checks. Already-current results now distinguish deferred maintenance, and a final reporting failure no longer rolls back an already verified installation, although incomplete finalization still exits unsuccessfully. Update work has no implicit deadline when `--timeout` is omitted; probes and recovery checks remain bounded, and stalled work may require cancellation. These driver changes apply when the corrected updater is running.

<Accordion title="Validate source updates before replacing the running build">

[Source updates](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) rebuild incomplete or stale runtime files even when the source commit is already current, then verify the activated build in a fresh process. Build-lock and worktree-reservation checks catch more blockers before stopping the Gateway. Update checks fetch the intended upstream without downloading unrelated branch and tag histories.

If an older updater fails before candidate code runs, use the documented [source-update procedure](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods) from an external terminal, stopping and starting through the actual service owner. Do not release a retained build lock until associated compiler processes, including detached descendants, have stopped. Retained Git rollback preserves local work and keeps its backup when source restoration is refused; a usable reflog may be required, and the Gateway can remain stopped for manual recovery. These checks do not cover every concurrent edit or undiscoverable service.

<Accordion title="Keep updates on the intended Bun or Node installation">

With this release’s updater, Bun-only installations can run update, repair, and Doctor without adding Node, while preserving a managed Gateway’s installation and explicit runtime pin. Follow the [Bun compatibility guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility) for first installation, including `OPENCLAW_PACKAGE_BUN_LAUNCHER` pointing to an absolute Bun 1.4+ executable, direct Bun launch, and the `--runtime-path` pin. Published updaters through 2026.9.6 retain their Bun-only first-hop limitation. npm plugin installation, updates, and removal now use bundled npm under Bun; Git/development tooling still has separate Node requirements. Recursive Bun configuration readers and lost synchronous results are also corrected.

A direct-Node managed service can select a supported replacement when its saved Node executable disappears; custom wrappers require their own runtime repair. Run Doctor or repair from the Gateway's installation when the CLI comes from another one. Bun headless nodes can discover releases without Node, but preparation still needs npm. That discovery uses the public npm registry, so private-mirror operators must keep it synchronized or disable `nodeHost.autoUpdate.enabled` and use their normal update workflow.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Repair plugin installations without losing their files">

Doctor preserves existing plugin files until replacement records commit, completes eligible deferred migrations after installing missing plugins, and repairs [archive-installed plugin links](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/plugins/install) without needing the original archive. Registered repairs follow release-selection policy, and native capture fixes avoid false WhatsApp source-change errors while retaining checks for actual edits. Rehearsals defer declared external migration resources, including Reef identity files, instead of modifying them in the live installation.

Older updaters blocked by the affected `import.meta` dependency parse error may need the documented [disable, update, and re-enable procedure](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/update-troubleshooting). Automatic update completion refresh writes core-only shell completions; run `openclaw completion --write-state` afterward to include plugin commands. Cleanup remains conditional on the files and processes Doctor can identify, and malformed migration records can still require repair.

<Accordion title="Recover retained conversations through Doctor">

[Doctor recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance) handles more retained conversation history, including verified transcripts without an old index, originals already contained in SQLite, and unchanged archives whose volume device number changed. Reimporting a recorded restored index preserves newer session labels, pins, and history. Relocation repairs retain the selected database and avoid recreating retired paths.

Stop the Gateway and preserve a verified backup before repairing history or eligible index-only corruption. Conflicting transcripts, unreadable sources, and unknown deletion history remain protected and can require explicit restoration before import resumes. Keep recovery manifests with their originals and follow Doctor's named recovery steps, including custom store paths. These repairs do not reconstruct arbitrary lost history or make every damaged database recoverable.

<Accordion title="Inspect and recover managed Gateway services">

Managed service checks distinguish an unknown inspection result from a stopped service and retain the selected installation across repair. Linux systemd recovery handles eligible stopped units whose loaded metadata disappeared; system-scope package updates still leave restart to the operator. A slow start can return `still-starting` with exit code 2 and `ok: false`, so recheck deep status before treating it as ready. Successful standalone [`doctor --fix`](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) can now start an eligible current-installation service that was already stopped, including one deliberately stopped. Update-time Doctor leaves activation to the updater, and installation-root drift stays outside that automatic-start path.

On macOS, updates can refresh an exactly recognized historical service PATH after backing up its definition. That migration can remove `~/bin`, `~/.nix-profile/bin`, `~/.volta/bin`, `~/.asdf/shims`, and fnm alias directories unless one supplies the active runtime. Affected CLI, MCP, or skill commands may need absolute paths. Edited or unrecognized PATH values remain protected.

<Accordion title="Preserve Windows service and update ownership">

Windows maintenance recognizes documented Scheduled Task defaults and retains the selected launcher and update identity more consistently. Stop and restart attempt graceful shutdown and retry recognized SQLite sharing errors, while Doctor avoids blocking its own service restoration with a maintenance lock. Restart results distinguish an accepted task launch from verified readiness, so inspect an unverified result before assuming the Gateway is back online. Updates stranded by an older driver may still need the installed version's `openclaw update repair`.

<Accordion title="Update containers and externally managed Gateways">

Container replacements can wait for a predecessor's startup lease instead of repeatedly failing immediately, but an unexpired lease can still delay startup for the remainder of five minutes. The default [container-worker image](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) is now `node:24.21.0-slim`. Offline nodes must preload it or explicitly select an already cached supported image; `node:24.19.0-slim` remains supported. A missing image fails visibly without falling back to an unisolated worker.

With a compatible job-capable OCM manager, the Control UI and internal `/update` can request an [OCM-owned update](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) and follow the same job through restart or reconnect. OCM supplies its verification and recovery instructions. Explicit Git targets and package dev-channel requests outside the manager contract are refused.

<Accordion title="Apply settings and repair only what can be confirmed">

Settings saved through the Gateway can apply without waiting for a file notification, and delayed duplicate notifications no longer force unnecessary rollback. If the state store is briefly busy before a reload starts, the Gateway retries while keeping the previous settings active. Failures after work begins are not automatically replayed. Channel transport edits preserve unaffected session rows and model choices, while relevant credential and model changes still refresh them. [Configuration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) also distinguishes an unreadable file from invalid settings and tells users when a rejected edit saved nothing.

<Accordion title="Settle work during Gateway shutdown and restart">

Gateway shutdown now waits for more configuration, recovery, and Activity recap work already under way, and prevents stale restart retries after their owner closes. Passive result, approval, and question waits release on disconnect or committed shutdown, allowing clients to reconnect and wait again without cancelling the underlying run or decision. Plugin reload and cooperative model-refresh fixes address specific shutdown wait cycles. These changes must be in the running Gateway before the restart; replacing its files does not change an older process already running.

<Accordion title="Inspect and clean managed workspaces deliberately">

Automatic [managed-worktree cleanup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) begins one hour after startup and avoids overlapping sweeps; frequent restarts can therefore postpone it. Use `openclaw worktrees gc` for immediate cleanup. Moved or unreadable checkouts are retained with clearer guidance, and session-cleanup dry runs avoid registry and verification writes. Worktree capacity uses a fixed 4 GiB reserve plus estimated writes, not a promise that 4 GiB alone is enough.

`openclaw worktrees recover-removal` can resume an interrupted ordinary clean removal from its exact original snapshot. Stop unmanaged writers first and allow space to reconstruct missing files. Separately, `retire-snapshot` permanently gives up an eligible managed restore entry before normal retention expires. An orphan retired without a snapshot also needs manual recovery in a separate checkout; preserving its files or branch does not recreate managed restore.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep database maintenance and history cleanup bounded">

Background session cleanup can progress while unrelated conversations keep writing, and archived conversations can load between cleanup units. Periodic database checkpointing and space reclamation run through existing workers, while checks on the selected history and retention rules remain in force. Clean same-version restarts can reuse verification only after the required checkpoint and close complete; uncertain shutdowns still need checks.

[Opening agent databases already using the current format](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) now uses narrower checks to reduce repeated startup work. Those opening checks can miss some transcript index or uniqueness errors and corruption involving shared or unused database pages. Doctor, daily full verification, migrations, and repairs retain full checks. Busy readers, genuine conflicts, and failed cleanup can still defer maintenance.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<Accordion title="Understand the Gateway’s resource use and failures">

[Gateway diagnostics](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics) add worker-pool and memory attribution, run identities in queue logs, and more useful database and update failure details. Monitor [`/readyz`](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/health) for recorded terminal state-database failures; `/healthz` remains a liveness check. Deep status and Doctor also identify a running Gateway whose Node executable has disappeared and advise a restart. Missing worker samples remain unknown, and ArrayBuffer memory is already included in external-memory totals.

Administrators can opt into allocation samples for collected objects or capture a [full heap snapshot](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics#full-heap-snapshot) for offline comparison. Full snapshots cover the Node main isolate, block the Gateway, cannot be cancelled once capture begins, and can need roughly twice the heap's memory plus disk space. Snapshots and comparison output are unredacted and may contain credentials or messages, so keep them private and delete them manually. Schedule a quiet capture window and avoid overlapping profilers; these tools diagnose memory use rather than fix leaks.

<Accordion title="See what happened during an update">

Update progress and saved history identify more of the work being performed, retain named warning and failure steps, and distinguish configuration, plugin, native-library, and service problems. Choose **Settings → Updates → Diagnose update** to ask for help; opening or reconnecting the dashboard no longer starts diagnosis automatically. Named administrators can review a sanitized failure report and submit its browser draft with their own GitHub account. Known upload failures preserve the prepared report for an explicit retry; uncertain submissions offer status checks rather than another submission. The detailed results remain available in [update history](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/status-and-history).

<Accordion title="Reduce repeated update preparation work">

Update preparation avoids repeated dependency scans, database copies, directory setup, and registry lookups, and can overlap independent file work. Savings depend on the installation, filesystem, and preparation step. Most begin with later updates driven by the corrected installed updater; candidate-side Doctor can also avoid duplicate advisory inspections when the older driver supplies the required private-copy markers. Required repairs, migrations, and final readiness checks remain.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow platform-specific FreeBSD maintenance guidance">

FreeBSD update refusals now identify the package-inspection operation and recognized error information more clearly. When service state is unknown, Doctor points to the platform's package-service or foreground workflow instead of suggesting unavailable CLI service management.

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover supported child work and remote workspaces">

[Restart recovery](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) distinguishes ordinary interruption from genuine failure and gives resumed parents a bounded list of current unfinished child runs to reconcile. Old or reset-session records remain history rather than instructions to replay. Children are not automatically relaunched, and the parent must confirm previous execution stopped and check uncertain external effects before repeating actions.

Graceful restarts also preserve node-backed cloud or paired-device machines and completed workspace edits for recovery and the next message. Interrupted tool calls are not replayed. This does not restore edits already discarded by older versions or establish equivalent recovery after abrupt machine loss or for SSH-backed sessions. Pending subagent completions stay tied to their original owner rather than being written into replacement state.

<details class="release-source-toggle">

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Show Interrupted status and give resumed parents up to 32 current unfinished children plus guidance to inspect the remainder [#159492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159492)

**Bug fixes**
