- Settle pending subagent completions against their original owner before cleanup [#159943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159943)

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Messaging

Replies are easier to follow across busy conversations, with fixes for formatting, attachments, delayed answers, and channel setup. Changes to bot defaults and webhook endpoints also come with specific upgrade steps below.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Read Telegram replies and code clearly">

[Telegram rich replies](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/rich-messages) retain status tables during partial streaming and render preformatted code without showing wrapper tags. Scheduled posts receive the delivering account's formatting guidance, aligned tables keep their spacing, and complete links to unsupported destinations show their labels instead of raw file URLs. With reply threading off, an implicit quote no longer forces a streamed answer to be deleted and sent again.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep quoted streamed replies in place when implicit reply threading is off [#155040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155040). Thanks @obviyus.
- Preserve rich `/status` tables and show unsupported control labels once during partial-stream finalization [#155088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155088). Thanks @pollybot13, @obviyus, @alekstodo, and @ruel225.
- Render standard preformatted HTML as code blocks in rich replies, retaining authored spacing [#157858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157858). Thanks @obviyus.
- Apply the delivering account's rich or plain formatting guidance to replies and announcements [#158028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158028). Thanks @obviyus.
- Give eligible scheduled posts account-specific formatting guidance and preserve table spacing around removed reply or audio directives [#159442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159442). Thanks @obviyus.

**Security and trust**

- Display labels for complete unsupported Markdown links without exposing their file destinations; bare paths and literal code remain outside this filtering [#137810](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137810). Thanks @chelsealong, @novaunboundai, @gaoanze888, and @xxg0dlessxx.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Follow Telegram delivery and retry outcomes">

Telegram keeps progress or shows a warning when a final answer fails to arrive, while preserving any parts Telegram already accepted. Check OpenClaw chat history before retrying an unconfirmed answer, since it may already have reached the chat and is not automatically resent. Final replies also handle repeated temporary rate limits and can finish after an unrelated plugin reload when the original bot, channel registration, and relevant settings remain unchanged.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move topic-binding storage off the message-handling thread while retaining ordered startup and shutdown [#156688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156688).

**Bug fixes**

- Retain progress or a delivery warning after a failed final answer, without replaying accepted text or completed tools; unavailable edits can leave old progress visible [#155906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155906). Thanks @obviyus.
- Coordinate bot-wide rate-limit waits and give final sends a bounded accumulated wait budget while previews yield [#157349](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157349). Thanks @obviyus.
- Recognize confirmed replies to the current forum topic, avoiding an extra final reply and missing transcript entry [#158054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158054). Thanks @sanjays2402, @obviyus, and @yangmanbobo.
- Keep conflicting updates ordered after a preceding handler fails while independent topics continue [#158573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158573).
- Close transport resources after failed webhook setup and retry polling errors with an HTTP 502 JSON-null response [#159258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159258).
- Hand completed final replies to the current Telegram runtime after unrelated plugin reloads, provided the original sender and channel remain valid [#160009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160009). Thanks @vacinc and @obviyus.

**Security and trust**

- Close membership-audit connections and refuse Mini App sign-in handoffs when owner access or bot configuration changes during the request [#160032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160032).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Use fresh connections for new text sends and withhold retries after ambiguous delivery failures, adding a connection handshake and leaving some uncertain sends undelivered [#156842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156842). See the [retry behavior](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/retry).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Move webhook callbacks onto the Gateway port">

Telegram, Feishu, Microsoft Teams, and Nextcloud Talk can receive webhooks on the Gateway's HTTP port. Use [Doctor's migration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-webhook-listeners) for old listener settings, update the external callback registration or reverse proxy, and verify incoming messages before setting `legacyWebhook` to `false`. Keep that explicit setting to leave the old port closed; removing it restores the inherited or default listener. Telegram polling and Feishu WebSocket mode are unchanged.

Follow the channel-specific instructions for [Telegram](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/transports), [Feishu](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/feishu/configuration-reference#gateway-webhook-route), [Teams](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/msteams/configuration), and [Nextcloud Talk](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/nextcloud-talk). Choose nonconflicting callback paths and distinct secrets for accounts sharing a route. A Teams route collision can also stop its old listener. On 2026.9.6 hosts, separately installed Telegram and Feishu plugins need distinct legacy endpoints per account. For Feishu installations using old host/port keys, update core before explicitly updating a pinned Feishu plugin; a core update preserves that pin.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Receive Telegram callbacks on the Gateway port while retaining the default `127.0.0.1:8787` listener until explicit cutover [#158732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158732).
- Receive Feishu callbacks on Gateway routes while retaining existing endpoints, including the default `127.0.0.1:3000` listener [#158861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158861).
- Receive Teams callbacks on Gateway routes with existing JWT authentication and port 3978 forwarding; migrate `webhook.port` with Doctor and resolve route collisions before cutover [#158939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158939).
- Receive Nextcloud Talk callbacks on Gateway routes, retaining `0.0.0.0:8788` until disabled; use distinct account secrets to avoid ambiguous routing during account shutdown [#159067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159067).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Restore separately installed Telegram plugin delivery and per-account legacy listeners on supported 2026.9.6 hosts [#159844](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159844).
- Bind Feishu legacy endpoints before reporting ready on supported 2026.9.6 hosts, with core-first migration guidance for old listener keys [#160455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160455).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Switch Telegram bots with clear queue consequences">

Switching a Telegram account to a known different bot no longer lets the old bot's message IDs suppress the replacement bot's incoming messages. This deliberately and permanently deletes the old bot's queued work, including pending messages, before the replacement webhook is ready. If startup fails, restoring the old token cannot recover those deleted messages. Rotating a token for the same bot retains its queue and duplicate protection; see [Telegram transport guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/transports).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Limits and compatibility**

- Clear old-account queue records on a confirmed bot replacement so overlapping update IDs can be accepted; same-bot rotations and unknown legacy identities retain their records [#158612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158612). Thanks @obviyus, @yangmanbobo, and @dankarization.
- Prepare bot identity before webhook delivery while retaining the permanent old-queue deletion and failed-startup recovery limitation [#159219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159219).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Slack conversations and progress usable">

Top-level Slack conversations in progress mode now default to a temporary reaction followed by one final answer, without a separate progress card. Explicit progress settings can retain previews, and finished cards show their completed state and link to the correct session. See [Slack messaging settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/slack/messaging) for the thread-only native-card exception and reaction controls.

Slack Socket Mode connections through proxies work again on Node; the separate Bun proxy limitation remains. [Verified Slack users linked to administrator profiles](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/slack/access-control) can also ask the agent to assign visible sessions to themselves, including sessions the agent created.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore Slack Socket Mode event reception through configured HTTP and HTTPS proxies on Node [#155841](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155841). Thanks @bill-starfoundry.
- Give ordinary Slack replies correct Markdown instructions for bold text and links while retaining native formatting for raw Block Kit fields [#155938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155938). Thanks @pash-openai and @drtony1.
- Keep repeated cleanup of an old webhook registration from removing its replacement [#158085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158085).
- Quiet top-level progress by default, retain explicit preview choices, and correct finished-card labels, accessible fallback text, and session links [#159657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159657).
- Carry verified linked administrator authority through session self-assignment from native Slack messages and app mentions, with visibility and revocation checks retained [#159694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159694).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Discord replies tied to their threads">

Discord preserves available quoted messages and restores reply references for bursts grouped into one turn. You can also [configure mentions separately in threads the bot starts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/groups#bot-created-threads), allowing follow-ups without opening the whole channel to unmentioned messages. This is opt-in, applies only when thread ownership is known, and requires the channel's documented event permissions.

Discord and Slack now allow messages from other bots to start turns when `allowBots` is omitted, including on existing installations. Set `allowBots` to `false` to keep the previous behavior. Existing access and mention rules still apply, and the bot-exchange guard does not prevent every possible loop.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move Discord conversation-binding storage off the Gateway thread while preserving account ordering and accepted shutdown work [#156627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156627).
- Add `requireMentionInBotThreads` on supported channels so bot-created threads can use a separate mention rule [#157713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157713). Thanks @seaurching.

**Bug fixes**

- Accept emoji-containing Discord Activity titles within the existing 80-code-point limit [#156096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156096). Thanks @superdiaodiao and @obviyus.
- Remove late-created progress drafts when queued follow-ups settle; settlement alone does not confirm final delivery [#156186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156186). Thanks @siri1410, @obviyus, and @revision-co-ltd.
- Restore direct Discord REST lookups under Bun without changing proxy selection [#156965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156965). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Restore native reply references for grouped incoming messages in batched reply mode [#158886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158886).
- Learn the Discord Activity application ID supplied at startup before checking availability [#159842](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159842).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Preserve Discord parent-message context and change omitted Discord/Slack `allowBots` settings to enabled, with explicit opt-out retained [#157091](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157091).
- Restore Discord plugin loading and direct API compatibility with supported OpenClaw 2026.9.6 hosts [#159773](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159773).
- Restore Discord voice loading on 2026.9.6 hosts, retaining profile-only context there rather than the newer multi-session instructions [#159831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159831).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve Matrix login and approval behavior">

Matrix reads saved credentials without blocking other Gateway work, and logout handles configuration changes without marking a restarted account as stopped. Authorized reviewers can also use the advertised reactions on [Matrix system-agent approval prompts](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/matrix/rich-messages), including prompts restored from saved state.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Move operational Matrix credential reads to workers and preserve completed logout results without stopping successor accounts [#156267](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156267).
- Accept system-agent approval reactions and restored targets under the existing approver rules [#158164](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158164).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Feishu tool results accurate">

Feishu reports only successful cleanup operations and returns the cell IDs needed to continue working on a newly created document table. Matching rich-text posts without attachments also stop starting duplicate agent turns when reconnect delivery gives them fresh message IDs.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Suppress matching attachment-free rich-text redeliveries after reconnect while preserving distinct posts and topics [#152679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152679). Thanks @leilei3167, @obviyus, and @yetval.
- Correct Feishu cleanup counts and ordered table cell IDs; make failed Matrix snapshot restores settle and close storage, and pass resolved configuration to Matrix profile and verification operations [#158923](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158923).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Pair and send WhatsApp media through the intended route">

Repeated WhatsApp login requests stop returning QR codes from attempts that already finished or failed, and status keeps useful connection warnings alongside authentication problems. [WhatsApp media uploads](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/whatsapp) also work through configured HTTP and HTTPS proxies again, respecting each upload destination's `NO_PROXY` setting. An invalid selected proxy route fails the upload instead of silently bypassing it.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Retain verified WhatsApp participant identity in new opt-in execution audit records without backfilling historical unknowns [#156730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156730). Thanks @joshavant.
- Release QR-login deadline timers when login completes or the QR code refreshes [#158272](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158272).
- Restore media uploads through HTTP/HTTPS proxies with per-destination bypass and certificate handling [#159851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159851). Thanks @joshavant, @choury, and @yangmanbobo.
- Start fresh login attempts after settlement and preserve recorded connection warnings without alarming untouched first-time setups [#67820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/67820). Thanks @mcaxtr.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep iMessage routing and diagnostics current">

iMessage conversations already assigned to an agent use that assignment before ordinary agent selection, and checks on uncached message references no longer query Messages on the Gateway thread. Logs also stop treating one known harmless Contacts diagnostic as an error while preserving genuine failures. If the conversation-binding service is unavailable, routing stops rather than guessing another agent.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Honor existing iMessage conversation assignments before ambiguous or conflicting ordinary routes [#123159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/123159). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Move uncached reply and action reference checks to the Messages database worker while retaining conversation checks [#155981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155981). Thanks @summitoperations26.
- Keep the known harmless Contacts reconciliation message in verbose logs while preserving surrounding or unrelated errors [#156334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156334). Thanks @olli0103, @altaywtf, @prashantkamani, and @omarshahine.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover Mattermost commands and thread reactions">

Mattermost can recreate an outdated slash command after a successful deletion whose response is interrupted. Reactions also reach the conversation containing the reacted-to thread message when its metadata resolves, following your existing threading settings. A failed lookup retains the parent-conversation fallback.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Continue slash-command recreation after a successful deletion loses its unused response body [#149458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149458). Thanks @masatohoshino and @altaywtf.
- Route reactions using the resolved message's thread rather than always using the parent conversation [#155812](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155812). Thanks @wangmiao0668000666 and @obviyus.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Connect and route the intended channel account">

Channels now require explicit configuration before connecting, unless a foreground Gateway uses `--ambient-channels`. If a channel previously worked only because its bot token was in the environment, [add its channel configuration](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/running); managed services need this configuration because installation does not retain the flag. Configured channels can still read environment credentials. Existing LINE, Discord, and Slack conversation assignments also take precedence over otherwise ambiguous agent selection.

Choosing Disabled in [Twitch setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/twitch) now blocks chat, but older empty-role configurations can remain open. Reselect Disabled or set `allowFrom` to `[]` if you intended to deny access. Open and Disabled discard saved user IDs, so switching back to Allowlist requires entering those IDs again.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Move Zalo Personal credential operations off the Gateway thread, reject stale QR setup writes, and wait for saved logout before replacement login [#156003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156003).
- Accept correctly bracketed Tlon IPv6 origins and remove downloaded MMS files when access is revoked before dispatch; private IPv6 still requires its existing opt-in [#157825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157825).
- Make Twitch Disabled setup deny chat using the existing empty allowlist rule, preserving mention requirements and explicit legacy repair [#157881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157881). Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Route bound LINE direct and group conversations to their selected agent without requiring a channel-wide default [#159116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159116). Thanks @sunlit-deng, @obviyus, and @superdiaodiao.
- Resolve Discord and Slack bindings before ambiguous agent selection and retain the bound agent's main-session identity; retained Slack bindings can still select an agent removed from the roster [#159533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159533).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require authored channel configuration or process-level ambient-channel opt-in rather than treating inherited bot tokens as consent [#157387](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157387).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Show useful activity while agents work">

When the main assistant resumes after waiting for a subagent, supported channels show typing again during active work, respecting your typing settings. Successful waits keep their commentary without false failure messages. With [tool progress enabled](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/progress-drafts), recent activity and plans remain readable after failed tools, and a failure can reach the preview before final-answer cleanup.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve commentary and avoid false abort errors after a successful wait for a child agent [#154881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154881). Thanks @romneyda.
- Let old named-tool failures roll out of Telegram and Slack progress while retaining plans, recent activity, and priority approval/error states [#156649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156649). Thanks @obviyus.
- Restore Discord typing for resumed parent work through its guarded typing adapter [#157271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157271). Thanks @obviyus and reporter Fabio Ponta.
- Retain completed native Claude CLI Bash command details in Telegram's existing opt-in progress display while hiding unconfirmed MCP commands [#158636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158636). Thanks @obviyus.
- Avoid overlapping provider typing requests when a keepalive restarts before its earlier request finishes [#158830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158830).
- Flush visible failed-tool status before final-reply cleanup without changing quiet mode or rolling retention [#158970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158970).
- Start typing when a public top-level parent actually resumes execution, using its configured cadence and stopping on completion, cancellation, or lost authority [#160039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160039). Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve attachments and reply context">

Replies can [resend available attachments from conversation history](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/rich-output-protocol) while keeping their captions and reply targets. Successful uploads to the requesting chat also count as answers without redundant fallback text. Telegram reply staging now uses its existing 100 MiB default instead of an accidental 5 MiB cutoff when no smaller limit is configured; Telegram's own upload-method limits still apply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve recognizable comparison prose in affected plain-text replies without removing text between comparison operators [#152671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152671). Thanks @leilei3167, @obviyus, and @yetval.
- Count successful final uploads, attachments, and effect sends as replies only when their destination evidence matches the requesting chat [#156807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156807). Thanks @joshavant.
- Keep later attachments from inheriting the first file's legacy URL; already-canonical saved metadata is unchanged [#157822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157822). Thanks @yangmanbobo and @obviyus.
- Resolve stored inbound attachment references for resending, retaining existing file-read and sandbox restrictions [#158720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158720). Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski and @obviyus.
- Apply Telegram's existing default media limit during reply staging while honoring smaller configured limits [#159491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159491). Thanks @sunlit-deng, @obviyus, and @kassol.

**Improvements**

- Move managed-image writes and cleanup off the Gateway thread and wait for accepted attachment metadata before publishing affected messages [#159348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159348).

**Documentation**

- Explain the host-side `ffmpeg` requirement for voice-note conversion, Feishu's attachment fallback, and WhatsApp's failed-send behavior [4383e8f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4383e8f1584969ead7cc2a854423a3c50c5db17d). Thanks @vincentkoc. See [voice output requirements](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tts/output).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Get child results without losing the main conversation">

Native child follow-ups can return their answer after waiting for delegated work within the same running Gateway. Native Codex can also deliver side answers in Discord and Slack while the original work continues, even with commentary and streaming off. Delayed replies keep their original conversation, and a reset or replacement stops results that have not started sending. These completion paths use native execution after Tasks retirement; see [session messaging](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-tool).

Agent-spawned persistent workers now require a separate child thread, as supported by Discord and Matrix. On Telegram and other channels where that would occupy the current conversation, use one-shot `mode="run"` instead; `thread=true` is rejected and Telegram's saved `defaultSpawnContext` has no effect. Deliberate ACP binding commands remain available. [Thread-bound session guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/thread-bound-sessions) explains the distinction and retained child history.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep quoted and forwarded context attached to each reply that steers an active embedded response [#145338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145338). Thanks @fransqaas.
- Finish an explicitly requested, successfully delivered reaction-only answer to the current message without extra fallback text [#156790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156790). Thanks @joshavant.
- Preserve a native child follow-up's final result across accepted yielded continuations; the historical Task-receipt implementation is superseded by native execution ownership [#156919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156919). Thanks @jalehman.
- Keep delayed self-replies in their original conversation and retain completed queued results across in-place restart; unfinished runs are excluded and backup restoration omits pending delivery records [#156984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156984). Thanks @joshavant and @vincentkoc.
- Mark later rejected or aborted subagent-result chunks as incomplete and stop whole-result retries that would duplicate sent parts; silent non-send outcomes are not comprehensively detected [#159185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159185).
- Deliver independent native Codex side answers without suppressing the original task's later final reply in Discord and Slack [#159416](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159416).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Restrict agent-created persistent bindings to child threads and ignore legacy system bindings on current-chat placements while preserving their records and history; later adapter-placement changes remain a routing limitation [#158992](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158992). Thanks @obviyus and @joshavant.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Approve authorized changes from supported chats">

Authorized users can approve or deny proposed OpenClaw changes in the requesting messaging chat, using supported buttons or `/approve`. Channel approver rules still apply; where none exist, approval requires the configured current owner. A free-text “yes” does not approve a change. Reaction approvals also work on affected iMessage, Signal, and WhatsApp prompts, with configured forwarding rules retained. See [approval commands](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/slash-commands).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Deliver delegated change approvals and their recorded outcomes to the requesting chat, with Allow once and Deny choices and current-authority checks [#157947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157947). Thanks @obviyus.
- Restore system-agent approval reactions on iMessage, Signal, and WhatsApp, including persisted iMessage targets; Signal forwarding reactions remain opt-in [#159132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159132).

**Improvements**

- Coordinate native approval-handler startup retries with Gateway wake-up and shutdown while retaining the existing retry delay and approval policy [#160315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160315).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep incoming messages ordered through failures">

Incoming-message queue storage now runs off the Gateway thread while retaining message order and duplicate checks. Queued messages follow current conversation rules before processing begins, and an uncertain database write is reported without blindly repeating it or claiming success. During shutdown, accepted outcome writes retain their message ownership until they settle, which can leave shutdown waiting if storage does not finish.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce database queries and temporary allocations when pruning large channel backlogs without changing retention rules [#159050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159050).
- Move operational incoming-message queue reads and writes to workers and prevent a released lane head from being overtaken [#159073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159073).

**Bug fixes**

- Keep message claims owned through pending completion, retry, and failure writes during shutdown [#158637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158637).
- Stop expired channel repairs before commit and surface uncertain writes without automatic replay or false durable acknowledgment [#159161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159161).
- Recheck current conversation-lane eligibility before a queued claim commits, leaving newly blocked messages pending [#159197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159197).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover eligible queued replies after restart">

Completed replies already held by the delivery queue can recover after a recognized restart when they are wholly unsent and their original owner still has permission. Delayed session results also keep their original account, recipient, and thread. A crash before queue admission cannot recover an answer from chat history alone, and partially sent or uncertain attempts do not gain automatic replay. Review the [storage and backup limits](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) before a downgrade; older readers can leave newer deliveries pending, and restoring a backup does not restore pending delivery records.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Recover eligible queued replies after recognized restarts while rechecking original-owner authority; removed grants do not revive old queued authority when restored [39183ae](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/39183aef1d3eab85b8b46753270853ba16867ba6). Thanks @joshavant.
- Preserve captured, wholly unsent final replies interrupted by update restart before dispatch [#156102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156102). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Keep delayed cross-session continuation destinations and reject results for reset or replaced requester conversations [#156802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156802). Thanks @joshavant.
- Preserve earlier delivery-retry wakes across clock rollback or stale reads while rechecking current queue eligibility [#159275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159275).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="See the actual channel status and failure">

[Channel diagnostics](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/channels) distinguish installed-but-disabled plugins from missing ones, show actual Gateway request rejections, and include relevant runtime failures in filtered logs. Rejected status requests now return a nonzero exit code for scripts. Error replies also identify recognized local worker timeouts instead of blaming the model provider, helping you troubleshoot the right component.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Identify local worker timeouts in channel warnings without changing genuine provider HTTP errors or retry policy [#155675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155675). Thanks @gokay-ai, @obviyus, and @endeavorpioneer.
- Report installed disabled channel plugins as installed without activating or reinstalling them [#159242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159242).
- Preserve Gateway rejection errors and return failure instead of a successful config-only status fallback [#159243](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159243).
- Include canonical channel runtime namespaces and descendants in filtered text and JSON logs [#159244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159244).

**Improvements**

- Skip unnecessary local migration preparation for explicit-channel message commands handled by a reachable Gateway [#157448](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157448). Thanks @wlassalle724.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Give voice sessions the right agent context">

Talk, Discord, FaceTime, and Voice Call share instructions explaining that a call is part of an agent's wider work. When consultation is available, those instructions direct questions about other sessions to the agent instead of treating the call as its only conversation. They do not give the voice model direct access to other sessions. Talk also skips redundant confirmation for read-only Code Mode lookups while retaining the normal checks for shell commands and changes. Voice Call's `maxChars` now limits profile files only, so configured identity and the shared instructions can make the total longer; see [voice context settings](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming#agent-voice-context).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid redundant outer voice confirmations for read-only Code Mode lookups while preserving confirmation for shell commands and mutating nested actions [#159474](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159474).
- Share agent-context instructions across realtime voice, retaining the paragraph when optional profile context is disabled; `toolPolicy` set to `none` still prevents consultation despite that guidance [#159548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159548).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Apply supported channel settings without losing work">

Changing channel settings during a conversation no longer leaves replacement channels attached to work that already ended, fixing a reported Matrix failure after reload. Google Chat also applies reloaded reply-visibility settings to the next incoming message without restarting. Credentials, callback routes, and other startup-owned settings retain their existing restart requirements.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep replacement channels independent of completed configuration-writing requests and preserve the current Gateway's owner across in-process restart [055db0c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/055db0c631bfb01598ae311015e928013bccdd4c). Thanks @ycmjason and @vincentkoc.
- Read current Google Chat reply-visibility settings for the next runtime-owned incoming turn without replacing explicitly scoped monitor configuration [#160323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160323). Thanks @rileyjjy, @obviyus, and @scientificprogrammer.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prepare interactive messages accurately">

Button messages that already fit a channel's capacity avoid duplicate preparation work while retaining the same controls and fallback text. Existing row limits, invalid-button handling, and overflow rules stay in place.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip duplicate global button preparation when the authored controls fit within channel capacity [#157050](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157050). Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Memory

Memory updates help OpenClaw find relevant notes, keep them current, and carry the right context into longer conversations.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Find memories across local models and mixed-language questions">

[Memory search](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) can keep returning keyword matches when an optional embedding provider fails, provided a usable keyword index is available. Explicitly selected remote providers still report errors. Local-model searches also accept two common argument spellings that previously caused rejected calls, while mixed Chinese-English queries retain English terms and Active Memory recognizes supported Russian questions about past conversations in its default mode.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Initialize Memory Core retrieval during idle time so later requests can reuse the warmed worker, without starting indexing or embeddings [#158563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158563)

**Bug fixes**

- Return indexed keyword matches after optional query-embedding failures, including exhausted retries [#157032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157032) — @myagizmaktav, @team-contractorscale
- Accept `min_score` and `max_results` in memory searches while preserving canonical-key precedence and other validation [#158308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158308) — @obviyus, @blackdiamondcyber-png, @he-yufeng
- Keep English technical terms in Chinese queries without spaces between languages [#159076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159076) — @yigtwxx, @takhoffman
- Recognize supported Russian past-conversation questions for default-mode deep recall [#159785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159785) — @ericcaiwx-star, @obviyus, @alexph-dev

**Documentation**

- Repair links to the Active Memory policy-disabled and first-recall-timeout troubleshooting sections [#156899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156899) — @linzeshi

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep memory indexes current with less repeated work">

With memory watching enabled, edits made during an indexing pass receive a follow-up pass instead of waiting for another operation to refresh them. Large folders configured as [extra memory paths](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/memory-config) avoid more unnecessary scans from unrelated file changes, and long-conversation indexing retains less unused message data. Batched updates and workspace-specific reads also avoid repeatedly processing unrelated transcripts or workspace records.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Release references to completed indexing vectors while later files are still being written, preserving shared cached vectors [#156173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156173) — @vincentkoc
- Select transcripts for multi-session updates in one pass while preserving target order and filters [#158364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158364)
- Limit built-in SQLite provenance and Dreaming reads to the selected workspace, so malformed records elsewhere do not block healthy data [#159190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159190)

**Bug fixes**

- Index edits received during an active pass and let folder scans yield to other work [#157163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157163) — @vincentkoc
- Retain only needed event data during long-session indexing and check recall reset boundaries without loading message bodies [#157278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157278) — @sahilsatralkar, @jalehman, @conan-scott
- Filter unrelated extra-path deletes and renames, scan shared roots once, and skip directories proven irrelevant to configured patterns [#158998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158998) — @nianjiuzst, @obviyus, @s1gv4rd

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve Dreaming schedules and readable memories">

[Dreaming](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/dreaming) no longer feeds nested reflections from its inline daily-note reports back into new recall entries. Previously stored generated entries remain unchanged. Newly promoted long snippets also prefer complete words and show an ellipsis when shortened, making saved memories easier to read.

If OpenClaw detects historical Dreaming schedules, run `openclaw doctor --fix` when prompted. Normal schedule creation and updates wait for that repair, which backs up the store and preserves a valid surviving job's identity, history, and retained authored options. Custom tagged jobs remain intact, while ambiguous or malformed entries remain with warnings.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Use the configured memory owner for Dream Diary reads and maintenance writes when older clients omit an agent, while retaining explicit selection and ambiguity errors [#156437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156437) — @orionation, @sundaydriver, @cyb3rb1ade
- Prefer nearby sentence or word boundaries and mark shortened deep-consolidation snippets without rewriting existing entries [#157322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157322) — @yun-0000, @ada-kvr, @obviyus, @holny
- Preserve historical Dreaming jobs for explicit Doctor repair and remove recognized duplicates only where a valid survivor exists [#157616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157616)
- Exclude nested inline Dreaming reflections from new daily recall while preserving surrounding user notes [#157879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157879) — @obviyus, @mariorivas1, @eric-basketbot

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Search and import Memory Wiki content">

[Memory Wiki](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/memory-wiki) searches and page reads skip unused link processing on link-heavy pages while preserving matching, ranking, and returned content. Open Knowledge Format imports also leave links inside code examples unchanged, including nested fences and multiline code spans, while ordinary links continue to be converted.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip unused Markdown link extraction during Wiki queries while retaining link graphs for compilation and lint [#152487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152487) — @kchuang1015, @context-down, @etzelm

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve literal links inside fenced, indented, and inline code during OKF imports [#159262](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159262)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep retained context and compaction boundaries accurate">

Long conversations receive focused [compaction](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction) fixes. Affected later summaries keep already-compacted conversation out of their input while retaining older history, and messages arriving as compaction finishes use the current session state. When a built-in run times out, its automatic compaction recovery stops too, without undoing history already committed.

OpenClaw-managed Desktop Codex Computer Use sessions also retain configured persona and memory-plugin guidance. Restart the Gateway to load the updated plugin. This applies to supported managed direct connections, and parent-local guidance is not automatically inherited by native child agents.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve selected memory-plugin and persona guidance in managed Desktop Codex Computer Use turns, including deferred recall tools [873dbf9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/873dbf97afbda5524b8e5f4617dcf76da726c1ae) — @jalehman
- Refuse steering when compaction state cannot be read, queue chat follow-ups, and continue cancellation checks for other eligible runs [#154868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154868) — @sxh313, @obviyus, @yunligou711-commits
- Let completed embedded-harness turns reach durable context engines through cache-TTL bookkeeping markers, without changing the separate pending-commit drain behavior [#156611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156611) — @holny, @obviyus, @kaspnov, @superandylin
- Refresh incoming chat identity and metadata when compaction finishes during reply admission [#156858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156858)
- Cancel automatic overflow and timeout compaction recovery when the admitted run times out [#159105](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159105)
- Preserve reset and compaction boundaries in the tool-only safeguard fallback, keeping already-compacted messages out of later summaries [#160211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160211) — @obviyus, @neyudo

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Preserve independent indexes and archived answers">

Doctor now leaves nonempty or unreadable per-agent QMD directories alone, preserving configuration and indexes that standalone QMD may still use. Only empty directories are removed, and retained files do not block startup. This does not restore data deleted by older versions or bring back OpenClaw's retired QMD backend. Before removing retained files yourself, [back them up and confirm standalone QMD no longer uses them](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-builtin).

Recovering an archived child-agent answer also moves preliminary database checks off the main Gateway thread while preserving archive identity and integrity checks. Incognito keeps its existing process-owned storage without creating disk state for those checks.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve standalone QMD configuration, indexes, and mixed directory contents during update and Doctor cleanup [#158569](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158569) — @obviyus, @billverhelle
- Move durable child-answer archive discovery and registration checks to the history worker, retaining empty-store and Incognito behavior [#160238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160238)

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Skills

Skills are easier to use across conversations and working directories, with fixes for picking up edited instructions and returning to Workshop suggestions.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Load the right skills and refresh changed files">

Edited [skills](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) reach agents more consistently after folders are replaced or file watching fails. When continuous watching cannot recover, OpenClaw refreshes affected skills during preparation instead of reusing stale instructions. This fallback requires watching to be enabled. Managed worktree sessions also keep the configured agent workspace and canonical project checkout as their intended skill sources, so an edited copy in the execution worktree does not unexpectedly take priority.

Self-hosted Agents API users can opt into [skill discovery from executor directories](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-agent-harness/runtime-config). Install the skills and their dependencies on the executor first, then configure its directory paths. OpenClaw passes those paths without copying files or applying its per-agent skill eligibility filters to that discovery. Hosted sessions do not use the setting, and changing a nonempty directory list requires a session reset.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reuse an agent's discovered skills across execution workspaces while preserving precedence and refresh behavior. Initial discovery remains synchronous. [#158609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158609)
- Group skill-collision logs by source directory, with counts and example names. Some collisions now log at info level, and separate worktrees can report separately. [#158092](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158092)
- Reuse parsed YAML within freeform skill and hook metadata recovery, without changing the accepted syntax. [#158374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158374)
- Add optional self-hosted Agents API discovery from up to 32 unique absolute executor paths, retaining Gateway function policies. [#159363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159363) Thanks @sjf-oa and @sjf.

**Bug fixes**

- Restore eligible skill catalogs on Codex fallback connections and refresh edited or removed skills in ongoing conversations. Managed relay delivery stays parent-only; after incognito compaction, the current catalog is restored for the following request. [b875c537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b875c537be3b1a3816a1566a683ab91c6de7abb7) Thanks @Colton-Harris, @jalehman, and @serialx.
- Skip binary checks for excluded skills. The same change adds private, owner-authorized repair turns with bounded execution, without enabling the deferred whole-loop update-repair route. [df15ee3a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/df15ee3a68a0912a779fccd0032e315716b9a5a9) Thanks @RomneyDa and coauthor Peter Steinberger.
- Prepare pinned Library skills through background database reads while retaining selected revisions and rejecting late results for retired callers. [#148822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/148822)
- Refresh affected skill sources during preparation after watching loses coverage, while retaining healthy caches. [#155901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155901) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Avoid unnecessary skill refreshes from unrelated same-name child folders on affected Linux layouts. [#156201](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156201) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep observing later skill edits after folder replacement on non-polling Node Linux and Windows. macOS, Bun, and polling retain their existing transport. [#156324](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156324) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Keep preparation-time refresh active until watcher coverage is verified, and wait for retired watcher work during shutdown. Failed retirement remains unavailable, and `skills.load.watch=false` disables both watching and this fallback. [#157591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157591) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Close idle remote Skills subscriptions when Gateway shutdown begins. The fix applies once the updated Gateway is running; supervisors still need the normal shutdown allowance. [#157592](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157592) Thanks @Kimiyu-186.
- Preserve configured workspace precedence in managed worktree sessions, including normal precedence for a worktree deliberately configured as the agent workspace. [#158110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158110)
- Preserve prompt-cache identity and stable references to unchanged delivered skills across paired-node and cloud-worker turns. Changed content gets new paths, and old randomized transcript paths are not migrated. [#160179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160179)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Review and revisit Skill Workshop proposals">

Returning to [Skill Workshop](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skill-workshop/proposals) now shows new suggestions on the first visit back and removes Apply actions for stale ones. If a revision request fails, your saved instructions return to the editor for retry. Completed proposal history takes less repeated processing to inspect while preserving the full history, ownership checks, and existing approval rules. Comparison dates also make clear which saved version you are comparing against.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move Workshop storage operations off the caller thread while preserving proposal approval and recovery. Update rehearsal cleanup retains its copy if closing the copied database fails. [#156110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156110)
- Inspect completed proposals without taking target write leases, while retaining ownership and fresh file-integrity checks. [#157563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157563)
- Reduce repeated decoding of completed proposal history while retaining pending recovery and the final complete listing. [#158757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158757)

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve the original reason for a failed background review in operator logs while keeping revoked work stopped. [#157069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157069) Thanks @obviyus and @Conan-Scott.
- Correct the earlier English elapsed-time badge wording, subsequently refined to identify the saved comparison baseline. [#157536](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157536) Thanks @RomneyDa.
- Read saved review outcomes from the selected state database instead of the default store. [#157774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157774) Thanks @RomneyDa.
- Restore the Skills settings heading and group Search, Workshop, Create, and Import actions in a toolbar that wraps on narrow screens. [#157931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157931) Thanks @Patrick-Erichsen.
- Refresh suggestions on first return and restore saved revision instructions after failure. Failed list requests are not automatically retried. [#158208](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158208)
- Label English comparison badges “Changes since” to describe the saved baseline, without changing timestamps or comparisons. [#158250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158250)
- Clarify tool arguments for personal skills and workspace proposals, and explain rejected fields without echoing their supplied values. Existing list and search limits remain unchanged. [#158255](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158255)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Find and read available skills">

Large Skills menus in Chat and New Session do less repeated browser work when opening, and choosing a menu action no longer unnecessarily moves focus to the message box. Agents using Code Mode can also discover and read personal skills again through structured results. Existing permissions and result limits still apply, including omission of binary content and the contents of unselected supporting files.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Reduce layout work when opening large Skills menus and preserve menu-click focus behavior. The complete list is still constructed. [#159917](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159917) Thanks @vyctorbrzezowski.

**Bug fixes**

- Restore authorized personal-skill list and read results for Code Mode, retaining the 20-entry list, 32-file support manifest, and 16,000-character whole-text inclusion limits. Also remove a disposal callback that kept replacement plugin registries alive through an old registry. [#160213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160213) Thanks @VACInc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Understand limited security-scan results">

When a skill scan finds more matches than it can display, its summary now counts omitted matches correctly when detection rules overlap. This makes a dense report easier to interpret without changing the scan's detection rules or blocking policy.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Security and trust**

- Correct omitted dangerous-command match counts when literal and imported-alias detections overlap beyond the reporting limit. [#158906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158906)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Discover tmux sessions through the skill">

The tmux skill's discovery helper now shows clean session names and readable creation times, including names containing a pipe character. It also corrects the attached status for sessions with one attached client; sessions with multiple attached clients remain a limitation.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Native Apps

The native apps improve everyday conversations, from writing and attaching files to finding the right chat and keeping setup moving when something goes wrong.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Use the web conversation inside the native Mac window">

Full [Mac chat windows](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/webchat) now use the web conversation by default on compatible Gateways, including for existing users of the Native experience. Rich replies, message actions, side panels and conversation controls sit inside the familiar native window, with its sidebar, menus and Cmd-K palette. Quick Chat and iOS keep their Swift interface. Pending native drafts, attachments or queued messages keep the Swift conversation in place until resolved, and unsupported or failed web loads fall back with connection errors visible.

The sidebar now defaults to newest-created threads, with message previews, automation conversations and system conversations hidden. Use **View options** beside Threads to restore those views or sort by recent activity. These choices are saved per app profile. Selected conversations and important attention messages remain visible, and pins keep their order.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Find agents, active threads and common actions with the native Cmd-K palette [#159350](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159350)
- Supply the embeddable web conversation and its native-host coordination [#159739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159739)
- Share one titlebar row between the Mac window and conversation header [#159932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159932)
- Save Mac sidebar sorting, preview and background-conversation choices [#160141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160141)

**Bug fixes**

- Include declared plugin dependency assets in Mac app packages using shared dependency layouts [#156188](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156188)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Make the web conversation the full Mac window's default with native-work and loading fallbacks. A web-originated navigation race involving pending native outbox work remains [#159946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159946)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep Apple chat drafts and sends during startup">

On iOS, initial agent discovery can finish while you type or send your first message without replacing the focused editor, losing the draft or hiding the submitted message behind an older history response. Changing accounts or conversations does not carry the old draft into the new one. On iPad, resizing the window or showing the sidebar preserves the active chat and draft, with replies and the composer sharing the available reading space.

If [iOS setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios) cannot prepare offline data for a replacement Gateway, it keeps your setup code and existing endpoint and shows the failure. Resolve the device-storage problem before retrying.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve newly typed iOS characters during a reentrant screen refresh [7f43b35](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7f43b351149930f7db5b4545ad4df4693fe168fc) — Peter Steinberger
- Keep iPad chat and draft state while resizing or changing sidebar visibility [#156683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156683) — @solvely-colin, @vincentkoc
- Recover stored replies in Swift chat after temporary history overload [#156772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156772)
- Preserve iOS drafts during initial discovery and submitted messages during stale history refreshes [#159065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159065) — @joshavant
- Keep iOS typing and keyboard focus through the initial agent handoff [#159296](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159296) — @joshavant
- Retain the first accepted iOS send while automatic agent discovery completes [#159404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159404) — @joshavant
- Expose actual composer readiness to accessibility and respect disabled parent views [#159496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159496) — @joshavant
- Preserve iOS setup input and existing connection settings after offline-data preparation refuses replacement; overlapping setup-attempt races remain outside this repair [#160218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160218)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Read and attach content in Apple chat">

[Apple chat](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios) can attach everyday documents and audio, including PDFs, Office files, text and ZIP archives. Use **Attach**, drag-and-drop or Finder paste on Mac, and **+ → File** on iOS. Files retain their names and original bytes, while images still resize to JPEG. Message-size limits apply, and an oversized send keeps its draft. Attaching a format does not mean every model can understand it, and downloading incoming uploads from native history is not added here.

Replies preserve paragraph breaks, keep related narration and tool work with their answer, and show completed-message times and the recorded answering model when available. Native chat also fixes the scroll stall after repeated sends, while Mac agent names and text or emoji badges make conversations easier to recognize.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Avoid rebuilding unchanged native streaming reply snapshots during tool updates [#158492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158492) — @vincentkoc
- Skip unused word-reveal preparation for earlier streaming prose blocks [#158534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158534) — @vincentkoc
- Show completed-message times and recorded originating models, with exact-date accessibility details [#159366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159366)
- Show resolved Mac agent names and text avatars, and update Apple New Thread choices without resetting selection [#159528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159528)
- Attach documents and audio through Apple file pickers, drops and Mac Finder paste, with draft-preserving size checks [#159667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159667)

**Bug fixes**

- Stop a canceled native media operation before starting an unnecessary byte request [4368865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4368865c48b5f86b3274350c76d5349b95381261) — Peter Steinberger
- Prevent chat scrolling from stalling after repeated sends [#147770](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147770) — @vincentkoc
- Keep Apple narration, tool work and final replies grouped with their own input [#156944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156944) — @iwhatsskill, @solvely-colin, @vyctorbrzezowski, @vacinc
- Preserve preview and run metadata and display oversized tool-read ranges without crashing [#158509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158509)
- Handle oversized speech options, rebuild audio conversion after input changes and cancel stale chat-settings requests [#159386](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159386)
- Preserve paragraph breaks before lists and neighboring blocks in Apple replies [#159517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159517)
- Prevent malformed-health and extreme-usage crashes and retain native model-scope metadata [#159778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159778)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Recover Mac startup and preserve runtime choices">

Mac startup repairs target the crash reported with 2026.9.6, preserve the actual error when a named profile fails to start, and avoid crashes while reading malformed diagnostics. Enabling or refreshing an installed [Gateway from the Mac app](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/bundled-gateway) also preserves your saved runtime choice. If that choice is invalid, the app reports the error and requires an explicit reinstall with `--runtime` or `--runtime-path`. Disabling the Gateway still uninstalls its service and removes that saved choice.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Avoid falsely rejecting a valid Developer ID signature during Mac cloud-worker setup [#156301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156301)
- Show the original named-profile startup failure when no process is listening [#156441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156441)
- Address async-sleep paths implicated in Mac launch crashes and add a bounded ARM64 packaging audit [#156881](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156881) — @coygeek, @davidcittadini
- Handle reversed braces in CLI diagnostics without crashing [#158516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158516)
- Preserve saved runtime choices during Mac Gateway enable or refresh and require explicit replacement of invalid choices [#160127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160127)

**Improvements**

- Clarify Dashboard sign-in and connection-recovery buttons and allow dragging the surrounding background [#157096](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157096)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Record the temporary withdrawal of the affected 2026.9.6 Mac in-app update, which did not repair already-installed copies [01a8055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/01a8055a214bcc73dd1fcb89fd8036d7bbc79bf2) — Peter Steinberger
- Refresh historical 2026.9.6 feeds for rebuilt archives; feed metadata alone does not establish successful launch or same-version automatic recovery [#157194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157194)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Renew Mac sign-in and recover Keychain prompts">

For saved Gateways protected by Cloudflare Access, the Mac app can begin renewing sign-in before expiry while the app is active and you have recently used the computer. Renewal may open your browser and require sign-in; a successful same-account renewal keeps the Dashboard page and location while reconnecting the native connection. Failed attempts leave the current session usable until expiry. If you deny a [saved-Gateway Keychain prompt](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/remote), background refreshes stop asking for the rest of that app session. Choose **Connect** or **Reconnect**, or restart the app, to try again.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Stop repeated background Keychain prompts after denial while retaining credentials and an explicit retry path [#157951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157951) — @stevenlee-oai

**Improvements**

- Renew same-account saved Cloudflare Access sessions before expiry, with browser interaction when needed [#159381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159381)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Keep recognized voice input and hook settings clear">

Mac Voice Wake now uses your edits to the recognized transcript, and clearing the text dismisses the request instead of sending the original words. For Swabble operators, `serve` now honors the configured minimum transcript length and cooldown. Existing minimums, including the default of 24 characters, can therefore stop short commands from triggering hooks. A qualifying partial transcript can also consume the cooldown before its corrected final text arrives; the manual `test-hook` command remains exempt from the minimum.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep Voice Wake's pending message synchronized with edits and dismiss whitespace-only sends [#159031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159031)

**Limits and compatibility**

- Enforce Swabble's existing minimum length and cooldown, including their effect on short inputs and partial-to-final speech [#159406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159406)

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Pair Android devices and understand their permissions">

[Android setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/android) presents four optional permissions first and puts the rest under **Additional features**. You can skip every grant, and granting Camera or Location permission does not automatically enable that capability. Users with the necessary Gateway authority can review the phone's requested capabilities and choose **Approve access and continue** in the app; setup waits for effective access, with manual instructions available when approval is unavailable or fails.

Saved Gateways can have recognizable names through **Manage Gateways → Rename**, including while offline. Names stay on that phone, and clearing one restores automatic naming. Older app versions may discard them after a downgrade. Pairing also avoids interruptions caused by permission changes or another network becoming available while the original connection remains open.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Simplify optional setup permissions and offer Android Settings recovery for blocked requests [#157701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157701) — @iwhatsskill
- Offer explicit, authorized phone-capability approval during setup and verify effective access before continuing [#157830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157830) — @joshavant
- Save phone-local Gateway names without changing server identities or connection details [#159714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159714) — @iwhatsskill
- Explain nodes, paired devices, offline retention and overlapping device lists in Settings [#159732](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159732) — @iwhatsskill

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve pairing's credential handoff during setup permission changes [#157569](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157569) — @fuller-stack-dev
- Correct release and debug App Actions package metadata [#159060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159060) — @joshavant
- Preserve an open QR-pairing exchange when another network becomes available [#159719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159719) — @iwhatsskill
- Add spacing around Nodes & Devices rows while retaining their management actions [#159728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159728) — @iwhatsskill
- Keep the Gateway picker open while scrolling; use Back, an outside tap or accessibility dismissal to close it [#159744](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159744) — @iwhatsskill

**Documentation**

- Explain protocol compatibility, why release numbers need not match, and why pairing does not prove every feature works [#157467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157467) — @iwhatsskill, @tomdailey55

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Organize Android conversations and work pages">

Android's [Pages menu](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/android) now lets you open and pin existing work destinations such as Agents, Automations, Usage and Terminal. Settings lives on the footer gear, including offline, and is no longer pinnable. Other saved pins keep their order; old Settings pins are removed, and a Settings-only selection returns to the Home, Threads, Skills and Overview defaults.

Ordinary chats appear independently in Threads while genuine child conversations keep their nesting and inherited settings stay unchanged. **Threads → Automations** provides access to retained automation conversations without mixing them into ordinary chat lists or deleting their saved pins. Use the **Automations** work page to manage schedules.

<Accordion title="Keep Android drafts, attachments and replies usable">

Android gives writing a full-width field above compact controls, with **+ → Camera** for photos or videos attached to the original unsent draft. Camera access requires permission, and video also requires microphone permission. The trailing button starts Talk for an empty idle draft, sends when there is draft content, and stops an active run when the draft is empty. Replies keep final answers visible when completed work is collapsed, with counts that identify individual failed, blocked or unknown tools.

<Accordion title="Choose Android models and inspect context">

Android model search accepts multiple terms across model and provider names and tolerates small spelling mistakes in longer words, while version numbers remain literal. The Effort gauge follows your adjustment even with Fast mode on, whose status now has a separate bolt. Context usage has moved out of the composer to **Chat actions → Context**, adding a menu tap while keeping the existing token and cost details available with read access.

<Accordion title="See Android Talk setup failures clearly">

Android Talk keeps setup and provider failures in a dialog until you dismiss them, so the explanation stays long enough to read. Granting microphone permission from the first **Start Talk** action can now continue into Listening without a second attempt. Missing providers or invalid credentials still need to be corrected in Gateway setup.

The companion still requires a paired Android phone running OpenClaw and connected to the Gateway.

</AccordionGroup>

## Models and Providers

Model choices and account settings are easier to follow, with additional ways to run agents and generate video. These updates also address interruptions in long conversations and the work needed to prepare model requests.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Find GPT-6 Sol and Luna on eligible accounts">

GPT-6 Sol and Luna work through supported OpenAI API, ChatGPT, and native Codex routes for accounts with access. Updated discovery makes the models visible to eligible subscription accounts, while existing selections and the Astra setup default stay unchanged. Native reasoning choices follow your account's catalog. For the embedded OpenClaw route, select the model's OpenClaw runtime explicitly as described in the [runtime guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes); changing the model alone does not select that runtime.

<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Restore eligible GPT-6 Sol and Luna subscription discovery and native Codex execution [#155989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155989) — Thanks @fduch-stranger.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Use Opus 5.5 with explicit version choices">

[Claude Opus 5.5](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) is available in the Anthropic API and Claude CLI catalogs, and becomes the rolling `opus` choice and the default for fresh setup. Explicit Opus 5 selections and custom aliases stay pinned. Opus 5.5 always uses thinking, with medium as its default; hiding thinking only changes its display. Automatic PDF selection can choose 5.5 even when chat is pinned to Opus 5, while an explicit PDF model remains respected.

- Use Opus 5.5 for rolling defaults while preserving explicit Claude selections [#156093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156093) — Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.

**Bug fixes**

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Run sessions through Agents API">

The explicitly selected [Agents API runtime](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes) runs conversations in a persistent hosted Linux workspace, with supported reasoning choices, live web search, permitted OpenClaw tools, and saved command results. It can receive files and return deliverables on supported Linux storage, and ordinary turns receive workspace instructions and plugin context. It also supports separate restricted completions for dream diaries and other background requests; these do not forward token or temperature limits. An official OpenAI API key and access to the selected model are required. Hosted execution remains the default; [self-hosted execution](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-agent-harness/runtime-config) requires your own controller and a workspace at the matching absolute path, and excludes file transfer.

Use a new or reset session to adopt changed system instructions, tool declarations, model, credentials, or execution environment. Resetting or deleting the local session does not delete the remote history or workspace. Hosted transfers allow 5 MiB per file, 10 MiB total, and 50 files per turn in each direction, subject to channel limits; confirm that returned attachments actually arrived. This runtime requires a single-user Gateway and exclusive OpenClaw ownership of its session. It does not enforce prompt hooks' per-turn tool lists, and rejects configurations that restrict its native shell, file, or web capabilities. Choose another runtime when those restrictions or strictly tool-free background calls are required.
