- Avoid schema rescans caused by mistaking SQL expression endings for transaction boundaries [#158973](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158973).
- Route run events through existing session identities instead of repeatedly scanning full stores [#159742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159742).
- Reduce duplicate ownership checks within uninterrupted state-read work [#159876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159876).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Reuse dedicated-read ownership proof for less than one second, including after an out-of-band lock-sidecar replacement or directory-alias change; expired proof is checked before dispatch [#160139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160139).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Save session state without blocking unrelated activity">

Saving sessions and streamed replies moves more database waiting away from other Gateway requests. New conversations save their starting history and session record together, and large tool results need less duplicate processing. Individual saves can still take longer, and some storage work continues to pause other requests.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move selected session writes off the request thread and let foreground writes progress between cleanup units [#155631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155631).
- Move durable history and selected session-detail reads off the request thread [#155740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155740).
- Reuse valid integrity proof across same-process database workers while retaining conservative cold-open checks [#155785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155785).
- Move streamed transcript and runtime-report writes to workers while preserving commit ordering [#156095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156095).
- Reduce large-message storage preparation without changing transcript bytes [#158402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158402).
- Reduce host-side preparation for SQLite worker operations [#158655](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158655).
- Reuse warm agent-database execution context and reduce repeated host checks [#158750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158750).
- Create fresh canonical durable sessions with one atomic transcript-header and entry write; alias, fork, and Incognito paths remain distinct [#158771](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158771).
- Reduce host CPU spent publishing small agent-state changes [#158843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158843).
- Avoid transferring duplicate parsed tool calls and results to the database worker [#159449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159449).
- Copy prior session-update context only when a callback reads it [#159780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159780).
- Avoid unpacking unrelated large tool results during guarded writes and prior-answer matching, with clearer write diagnostics [#159898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159898).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Prepare independent conversations with fewer repeated checks">

Independent existing conversations can prepare replies concurrently through more paths, reducing waits behind unrelated sessions. Creating a conversation also avoids unnecessary history scans, and busy starts leave more opportunities for other requests to progress. Some workloads can still see longer pauses or take longer to start a reply.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Remove redundant session-metadata reads during chat admission [7dc4905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7dc4905d61a8544001c84c8794c6b24e985fd7d5).
- Move conversation-index publication and selected startup reads into workers [e0d8000](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/e0d80000067d224e93af6107ed093a21e046879f). Thanks @fuller-stack-dev and @joshavant.
- Move managed-worktree snapshot reads off the calling thread [#156315](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156315). Thanks @todddickerson.
- Share simultaneous approval-replay preparation for matching session and reviewer bindings, retaining per-client checks and bounded retries [#156358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156358).
- Batch worker-machine metadata refreshes through background database reads [#157079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157079).
- Skip device-storage setup for eligible implicit local loopback CLI calls with authentication disabled [#157166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157166).
- Read opt-in CLI timeline settings without opening shared state [#157252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157252).
- Reuse healthy discovery workers during session creation and expose slow-create phase diagnostics [#158433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158433).
- Avoid empty archive-recovery waits before replies when the committed database identity still matches [#158559](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158559).
- Reuse sandbox classification and avoid duplicate workspace-directory preparation [#158610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158610).
- Skip unnecessary archive preparation when adopting existing sessions [#158660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158660).
- Read targeted rows and label occupancy instead of unrelated session inventory during conversation creation [#158809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158809).
- Prepare personal-account authority once for concurrent chat work; running installations require profile changes through Gateway RPCs [#158941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158941).
- Yield between session-publication batches and combine pending activity recaps within the same session generation [#159102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159102).
- Allow audited operations on independent session keys to prepare replies concurrently while overlapping operations keep their ordering [#160183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160183).

**Limits and compatibility**

- Historical Task-owner startup persistence moved off the Gateway thread before the Tasks system was retired [#156064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156064).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Reduce repeated processing during live replies">

Live replies do less repeated text cleanup and preparation of unchanged tool details. Large progress updates also stop unnecessary processing once they exceed the existing replay limit. Reconnecting keeps the same retention limits and content.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Skip full runtime-context cleanup scans when recognized removable headers are absent [#153211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153211). Thanks @jjjhenriksen, @patrick-erichsen, and @noelillinger.
- Stop capturing oversized progress at the existing replay limit and retain compact tool metadata [#158572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158572).
- Reuse unchanged tool-definition projections while applying current diagnostic redaction [#158871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158871).
- Reduce repeated identity resolution and allocation in session-activity broadcasts [#159182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159182).

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Release retained resources after work settles">

Busy conversations retain less unnecessary data, and tool and plugin tracking does less memory-management work. This reduced long pauses in the measured concurrent-streaming workloads. Workspace cleanup also waits on storage without holding up other requests, while some idle workers stay available for reuse. Pauses can still occur.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Improvements**

- Move managed-worktree lease cleanup off the Gateway thread; the accompanying Task-backed maintenance settlement is historical after Tasks retirement [#156640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156640).
- Prepare worktree cleanup workers earlier; the accompanying Task-backed maintenance-start transition was subsequently retired [#156872](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156872).
- Retain one idle agent-database executor for up to thirty minutes, with cross-agent eviction unchanged [#158405](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158405).
- Release unneeded large event payloads retained by live-chat ownership checks [#158643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158643).
- Reduce plugin ownership bookkeeping behind long garbage-collection pauses during concurrent streaming [#159103](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159103).
- Reduce repeated tool-classification allocation during agent preparation [#159464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159464).

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Other Bug Fixes

These fixes address interrupted conversations, delayed results, and cleanup failures that could get in the way of continuing your work.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Database recovery and session storage">

Long-running installations avoid a source of temporary-file exhaustion that could block updates even with disk space available. Session lists keep refreshing during unrelated writes, and several database waits no longer pause other Gateway work. When a failed database operation also leaves cleanup stuck, the affected agent can accept the next request once that cleanup fault clears, without replaying the failed turn.

Busy installations can now retain up to 256 MiB of queued database input, up from 64 MiB; this is additional buffering, not a total memory limit. If an ambiguous `global` or `unknown` session address is refused, select an unambiguous conversation. Existing history is preserved, without an automatic merge or rename.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep archived-session search, terminal history, Talk, and shared PR updates current. [0bbad56f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/0bbad56fa80945db4323771873c9944e67454679) Thanks @galiniliev, @steipete.
- Keep history reads working during concurrent agent registration. [3d903819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d903819c5641ed8735aebe55c86844957009952) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Avoid database-worker contention during Gateway authentication recovery. [3f341061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f341061594210e824b05aa20826af23ec0c5c76) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Preserve concurrent session metadata while storage becomes ready. [526f67dc](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/526f67dc6382bae28cb26231b0813ce755521d80) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Release memory-pressure listeners after database-worker cleanup. [8a5386d6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8a5386d63e0472ad081c0533badde6a5a174394a) Thanks @steipete.
- Prevent temporary-file exhaustion from SQLite coordination, retain Cron result writes during shutdown, and stop retired Wear audio frames changing the current animation. [8b16e532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8b16e5328ecde96f7fe48aa637fec18cfde3fd47) Thanks @steipete.
- Explain when a database path disappears during access checks, without treating the file as recovered. [9512a28b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9512a28b6fe6520a5377d38a287494b70cf2e55a) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Release database access after a command reaches a closed worker. [a53728bd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a53728bd3b61b38049cb875103ed455cc44e87fe) Thanks David Stephens.
- Preserve stored session addresses, history, and family links through alias changes; reject stale queued writes. [#155886](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155886) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve saved sessions, ACP metadata, and existing literal parent links when the main-session alias changes; absent parents retain the existing alias fallback. [#156109](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156109) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Stop counting a completed request as active while background participant details are saved. [#156172](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156172) Thanks @steipete.
- End refresh loops for absent subagent runs and reuse unchanged run information. [#156268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156268) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep session lists refreshing while unrelated sessions are being written. [#156286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156286) Thanks @steipete.
- Avoid rebuilding unchanged session lists after identity-permission changes. [#156339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156339) Thanks @steipete.
- Reject session and Cron metadata invalidated while their database reader closes. [#156519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156519) Thanks @steipete.
- Restore sharing and transcript notes for custom session-store locators backed by symlinked databases. [#156684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156684) Thanks @steipete.
- Show another connection's committed database changes on the next independent read, preserving an active read snapshot. [#156824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156824) Thanks @vincentkoc, @steipete.
- Keep a healthy SQLite connection usable after a pre-commit automatic rollback; the failed write still fails. [#157241](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157241) Thanks @steipete.
- Save durable session activity through the database worker, preserving event order and stopping replay when a write's outcome is unknown; the first flush may take longer, and Incognito retains its existing handling. [#157598](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157598) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep older child-answer lookups and selected Cron database waits off the Gateway thread; accompanying Tasks and TaskFlow read paths were later retired. [#157634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157634) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Reuse valid same-process database integrity checks after the last writer closes. [#157666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157666) Thanks @marinabotobs, @steipete.
- Attempt tool-resource cleanup when agent setup fails, preserving the original error and reporting uncertain cleanup. [#157678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157678) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Resolve dangling workspace symlinks correctly during agent deletion and close File Transfer handles after identity-check errors; terminal uploads now remove some forbidden filename characters and use names such as `CON_.txt`. [#158008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158008) Thanks @steipete.
- Let Gateway requests proceed while session cleanup and cold-storage commits settle. [#158073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158073) Thanks @steipete.
- Prevent the reply-startup lock cycle caused by overlapping read-marker updates with a durable context engine. [#158209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158209) Thanks @iwhatsskill.
- Skip malformed non-object Claude CLI history rows and retain the original abort error for already-aborted node work. [#158228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158228) Thanks @steipete.
- Avoid chat-send lock waits when the needed device identity is already cached. [#158259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158259) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Allow session creation through database directory aliases such as macOS /tmp. [#158401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158401) Thanks @joshavant.
- Finish releasing a failed-startup turn claim after temporary database contention, allowing the next message without replaying the failed input. [#158486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158486) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev, @steipete.
- Share agent database access across directory aliases and reject queued writes after an alias is retargeted. [#158611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158611) Thanks @joshavant.
- Release completed-run diagnostic references after queued events drain. [#158949](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158949) Thanks @steipete.
- Allow the next request after a transient failed-operation database-cleanup fault clears, while explicit revocation remains terminal. [#159451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159451) Thanks @aspalagin and @obviyus; related issue report by @desksk.
- Keep persistent reply startup and Stop responsive during database admission waits, joining cleanup before a queued successor. [#159632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159632) Thanks @steipete.
- Restore the loaded conversation after a rejected nested transcript write, preserving selected branches and valid message ancestry. [#159807](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159807) Thanks @steipete.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Earlier asynchronous Tasks retention cleanup, superseded by the removal of Tasks and TaskFlow. [ac9b848d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ac9b848d2f0bb7126759d06046727cf1d24e4d5d) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep admitted chat writes attached to their original conversation and database; refuse ambiguous raw session aliases without merging history. [#156819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156819) Thanks @steipete.
- Give independent database workers separate request slots and raise their shared queued-input allowance from 64 to 256 MiB. [#157230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157230) Thanks @steipete; issue report by @todddickerson.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Subagent results and interrupted work">

A finished [native subagent batch](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/announce) can return its results while an unrelated earlier batch continues; delivery still waits for children and nested work belonging to that batch. Stop also prevents selected finished helpers from restarting stopped work when their result delivery retries. Restart fixes preserve an already-owned nested continuation's successful result and recover original requests hidden behind large internal history records.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Preserve successful child completion timing when publishing its reply. [53f7e5fd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/53f7e5fd46f5ac44b9d690549b73def4d7b526ba) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Reserve collector queue order before requester lookup and retain the original completion destination. [5cf18de3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5cf18de3d103a6bea797cbfe52fa8f51dce8c596) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Resume an agent after a clean sessions_yield instead of mistaking a stale synthetic message for cancellation. [66791fc1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/66791fc10e964525496b22be50013e694e51ffe2) Thanks @steipete.
- Finish failed-launch cancellation before deleting a collector session or advancing its queue, including restored queues. [d00524c2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d00524c295776a673d1450ed44b95021976539e3) Thanks @vincentkoc.
- Report cancellation before cleaning up a collector stopped during startup. [d65bc76d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d65bc76d60daaa16078748bd2f2a9c090564d2ee) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Wait for the original replayable reply before agent.wait reports completion; replay remains limited to the existing process-local cache. [#156078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156078) Thanks @joshavant.
- Prevent selected completed helpers from automatically restarting stopped work, retaining their results and allowing new human instructions. [#156124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156124) Thanks @shakkernerd.
- Release a failed restart-recovery attempt so the conversation can retry within its existing recovery budget. [#157214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157214) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve completion input through compaction and stop futile retries for the specific closed-turn transcript mismatch. [#157571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157571) Thanks @steipete.
- Limit repeated identical settlement-write warnings while keeping the existing retry cadence and reporting suppressed counts. [#157871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157871) Thanks @etzelm, @amesgit, @jalehman.
- Record native subagent and ACP terminal outcomes without blocking unrelated Gateway events during database contention. [#158118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158118) Thanks @steipete.
- Recover an interrupted request and saved tool results when large internal records obscure the loaded history window. [#158363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158363) Thanks @jalehman; related issue reports by @hannesrudolph and @simonspid.
- Refresh optional parent-session attribution after creation, replacement, or deletion. [#158581](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158581) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve a successful nested subagent result after restart when its exact pending continuation still owns the work. [#158585](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158585) Thanks @jalehman.
- Await orphaned-subagent database admission without blocking startup's main thread on integrity scans. [#158620](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158620) Thanks @fuller-stack-dev.
- Deliver a finished yielded batch without waiting for unrelated older work, retaining waits for its own nested children and cleanup. [#158642](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158642) Thanks @obviyus; report by @potterdigital and independent diagnosis by @sxh313.
- Avoid waiting again for results already being processed, show scheduled agents only permitted automation actions, and tolerate harmless session updates during publication-permission preparation. [#160036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160036) Thanks @steipete.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Earlier missing-Task completion retirement and inspection, superseded by Tasks removal; this historical change discarded delivery rather than restoring it. [#157061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157061) Thanks @steipete.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Canceled messages and follow-up turns">

Canceling or expiring a [queued follow-up](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) before it starts now leaves the active reply and its goal running. Messages arriving after a turn finishes go to the next turn, and stalled-run recovery can release queued work after repeated waits or model requests make no progress. Explicit [steering](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-tool) stays queued while a built-in agent is busy; acceptance confirms enqueueing, not that the model has read or saved it, and the queue does not survive a restart.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Route late follow-ups to the next turn after completion or handoff, while current-turn retries can still accept steering. [2fd61364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2fd61364420671efcd6b6c1d57bbd6e59b561863) Thanks @steipete.
- Prevent new input and reset confirmations from canceled or replaced requests and preserve recorded reset retry outcomes; already-adopted resets and child input may finish, and an unstarted follow-up needs a separate submission. [#150640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150640) Thanks @sallyom, @vincentkoc.
- Keep exact session-description requests moving during unrelated bulk refreshes, within existing capacity bounds. [#155471](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155471) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep one redacted failed-image recovery note across same-run retries without reinserting compacted history; disk-backed internal writes use a worker. [#155721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155721) Thanks @steipete.
- Retain the owning agent when resuming by session ID in partitioned custom stores; ambiguous shared-store sessions still require agent selection. [#155741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155741) Thanks @steipete.
- Move file-backed reply-recovery reads to workers, preserve follow-ups across compaction, and keep successor sessions from hiding an interrupted run's warning. [#155982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155982) Thanks @steipete.
- Report canceled queued chats as aborted to waiting and replaying clients. [#156638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156638) Thanks @joshavant.
- Complete accepted self-archiving after the agent finishes; revoked authority still prevents it, and pending archives do not survive a Gateway restart. [#156978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156978) Thanks Vincent Koc (reporter and requester).
- List saved conversation branches despite concurrent label or activity updates. [#157060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157060) Thanks @steipete.
- Recognize unchanged progress-card content when loop detection is enabled, without changing defaults or thresholds. [#157578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157578) Thanks @jalehman.
- Keep a canceled or expired waiting message from failing the active conversation, pausing its goal, or adding a false timeout entry. [#157958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157958) Thanks @steipete.
- Recover from unchanged wait loops and repeated model requests without semantic progress, retaining legitimate tool and provider-retry allowances. [#158428](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158428) Thanks @steipete.
- Keep accepted explicit steering queued beyond the sender's reply deadline and remove only its unconsumed guidance when the receiving run ends. [#160490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160490) Thanks @vacinc.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Command results and process cleanup">

A command that has already finished can retain its real result when a busy Gateway processes its completion late, instead of being mislabeled as timed out. Windows commands stay alive while their awaited cleanup settles, and Linux [background-process cleanup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) collects eligible adopted descendants that have already exited. Commands still running remain subject to their deadlines and operating-system permissions.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Keep terminal input and resize callbacks in the running CLI's context. [3067f9cf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3067f9cf01ae8b3ce3ec6bb5b80d58f89989ad48) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Request command-group termination before an oversized control message causes the relay to lose cancellation authority. [b8a66645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b8a666452976c2b922e2655eca54719591139ced) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Treat permission-denied process probes as existing processes and fully exited single-thread Linux zombies as dead, including eligible Doctor recovery. [#151107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151107) Thanks @ekinnee, @altaywtf.
- Keep Windows command cleanup alive until its awaited result settles. [#157042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157042) Thanks @steipete.
- Collect eligible adopted Linux command descendants through longer configured shutdown grace periods without taking unrelated exit statuses. [#157863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157863) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve observed command exits and output during delayed timeout handling; completed results may survive slightly beyond the nominal deadline. [#159578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159578) Thanks @steipete.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Earlier background-command registration and completion ordering protected fast-exit handles, exit results, and cleanup; its Tasks bookkeeping was later retired. [#157177](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157177) Thanks @steipete.

</details>

</Accordion>

<Accordion title="Worker workspaces and recovery">

Worker chats retain their workspace through automatic daily or idle conversation resets, and a stuck cloud worker no longer holds unrelated sessions behind the same queue. Workspace transfers also avoid the identified verification and temporary-file races. Change an existing session's workspace after its active work finishes; requests made while it is busy return a retry message. Explicit `/new` and `/reset` are outside the automatic-rollover repair, which also does not restore bindings already lost.

[Worker inference](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/worker) keeps accepted provider and storage work accounted for through Stop and shutdown. If a provider never finishes work that can still write data, shutdown can remain pending. Operations on the same session or shared environment can also still wait for one another.

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>

**Bug fixes**

- Identify the Docker or Podman operation and deadline in container timeout messages without displaying private arguments; check the selected daemon before retrying. [136cfdc7](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/136cfdc7a6cf0338a41ffb9523273d7f07a3b42f) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Keep inbound worker-transfer staging filenames stable during quota checks, preserving validation before publication. [a9760fd9](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a9760fd99f9700efc4f228a02d7680eb25c4b569) Thanks Peter Steinberger.
- Reject new checkpoint imports or publication after a cloud edit loses authority, preserving pending results for recovery. [#150611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150611) Thanks @sallyom, @vincentkoc.
- Keep admitted worker inventory usable after an independent database-open refusal, while terminal failure and quarantine still disable it. [#155875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155875) Thanks @meska, @vincentkoc, @obviyus.
- Complete worker workspace verification and result uploads without the missing `__name` helper error under source-loader transforms. [#156068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156068) Thanks @joshavant.
- Allow prepared-worker Git verification up to ten minutes per operation within the provider deadline, with specific failure diagnostics and unchanged integrity checks. [#156212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156212) Thanks @steipete.
- Move worker inference persistence off the request thread, save admission before acknowledgment, and join accepted writes and provider work during cancellation and shutdown. [#157422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157422) Thanks @steipete.
- Preserve worktree and repository-workspace bindings across automatic daily or idle resets, leaving explicit reset behavior unchanged. [#159506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159506) Thanks @chelsealong, @obviyus, @dimonnld.
- Let independent sessions proceed during stuck cloud-worker operations and begin Stop cancellation before final cleanup waits. [#160299](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160299) Thanks @steipete.
- Return a fresh node launch's invalid-request diagnostic without canceling work never registered; update the older Gateway or node component for a descriptor-version mismatch. [#160380](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160380) Thanks @steipete.
- Finish Crabbox teardown for qualifying absent leases and release warm-image allocation holds; coordinator Stop requires matching read and release absence responses for that lease. [#160571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160571) Thanks @steipete.
- Accept complete bundled CommonJS dependencies during cloud-session bootstrap validation; affected source installs need a rebuild and restart. [#160583](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160583) Thanks @steipete.
- Recognize Crabbox 0.67 lease-read query strings during absent-lease cleanup without treating inspection alone as Stop confirmation. [#160660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160660) Thanks @steipete.

**Limits and compatibility**

- Require an idle session before workspace changes or preparation callbacks, preserving its conversation history when adoption succeeds. [#157259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157259) Thanks @vincentkoc.

</details>

</Accordion>

</AccordionGroup>

## Maintainer and Internal Changes

This release includes 1,547 maintainer-only units covering contributor tooling, automated tests, release preparation and internal code maintenance.

<AccordionGroup>

<Accordion title="Engineering, tests and release-workflow maintenance">

For contributors and release maintainers, this work repairs test setup, reduces repeated compiler and test-planning work, and updates package qualification and release recovery tools. Internal refactors share existing implementations across channels, plugins and Gateway services. The complete list includes historical experiments, reverted changes and preparation work, with their limits kept alongside each source. Contributors can find current procedures in the [testing guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/help/testing/suites) and [local proof guide](https://docs.openclaw.ai/ci/local-proof).

<details class="release-source-toggle">
<summary>Sources and complete change list</summary>
